# Public API, CLI and UI surface (incl. extensions) — parity inventory (Argo CD v3.5.5)

Scope: everything a client touches on the `argocd-server` process and the `argocd` CLI: (1) every gRPC service and RPC defined in `server/*/*.proto`, with its grpc-gateway REST binding, streaming nature and authorization check, plus the non-gRPC HTTP endpoints multiplexed on the same port (badge, terminal websocket, proxy extensions, `/extensions.js`, swagger, healthz, static UI, CLI download); (2) every `argocd` CLI command documented in `docs/user-guide/commands/*.md` (170 files) grouped by command group, plus global flags, local config, transport selection, port-forward, core/headless mode and CLI plugins; (3) the web UI in `ui/src/app` (application/applicationset lists and details, resource panels, logs, terminal, sync/rollback, creation wizard, settings pages, user info, help, banners, custom CSS, analytics, status badge panel, deep links, external URLs, application notice) and both extension mechanisms (UI extensions and proxy extensions). Out of scope (covered by other areas, referenced only where the API exposes them): reconciliation/sync engine semantics, ApplicationSet generators, SSO/Dex/OIDC flows and session token issuance internals, the RBAC policy language itself, notifications engine services/triggers, repo-server manifest generation, source hydrator internals, webhook payload processing, metrics catalog. All permalinks are pinned to commit `a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c`.

Conventions used below: "RBAC `x, y`" means `enf.Enforce(claims, resource=x, action=y, object)` with `object` as stated; `<rbacName>` for applications is `<project>/<name>` when the app lives in the control-plane namespace, else `<project>/<appNamespace>/<name>`. Error codes are gRPC codes (REST maps them to HTTP via grpc-gateway: `InvalidArgument`->400, `Unauthenticated`->401, `PermissionDenied`->403, `NotFound`->404, `FailedPrecondition`->400, `Unimplemented`->501, `Internal`->500).

## Part A — Server API surface

[server/repository/repository.proto#L128](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.proto#L128), [server/session/session.proto#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.proto#L42), [server/settings/settings.proto#L100](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.proto#L100), [server/version/version.proto#L32](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/version/version.proto#L32)
- **Maturity**: GA (individual RPCs marked deprecated where noted)
- **Semantics**: The API server registers 13 gRPC services on one `grpc.Server` and the same 13 services on a grpc-gateway `ServeMux` mounted at `/api/` (REST/JSON). Every RPC below must exist with identical full method name (package names matter: `SettingsService` lives in proto package `cluster`, so its full name is `/cluster.SettingsService/Get`), identical HTTP verb/path template, identical body mapping (`*` = whole request message, a field name = that field is the body, `—` = no body; remaining fields bind from path and query string), and identical streaming direction. Paths with `{a.b}` bind nested fields; `{subresource=**}` is a multi-segment wildcard. Where two RPCs share one HTTP binding (deprecated `RepositoryService.List/Create/Update/Delete` vs. `ListRepositories/CreateRepository/UpdateRepository/DeleteRepository`) both resolve to the same server logic, so REST callers observe one behavior. The `events.proto` file defines only the `EventList` message (0 RPCs).

- **Edge cases**: duplicate REST bindings (rows 83/86, 93/94, 96/97, 99/100) must not error at registration; `GET /api/v1/applications` field projection via `fields=` (see API-003); `PodLogs` has two GET bindings; `GetManifestsWithFiles` is client-streaming and its REST binding cannot meaningfully stream a request body (CLI uses gRPC); deprecated RPCs remain callable over gRPC.
- **Configuration surface**: `argocd-server --port` (default 8080), `--address`; proto package/service names are a wire contract.
- **Exposure**: API (gRPC + REST); consumed by CLI and UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: for each of the 109 RPCs, a gRPC call and (where bound) a REST call reach the same handler and return the same JSON shape.
  - Negative: unknown REST path under `/api/v1/` returns 404/`Not Found` from the gateway; wrong verb returns 405/501 equivalent.
  - Authorization: each row's RBAC column — call as a user lacking the permission and assert PermissionDenied (or filtered list).
  - Recovery: server restart preserves no in-memory API state; streaming RPCs resume after client reconnect.

### API-002 Single-port protocol multiplexing (gRPC, gRPC-Web, HTTP/1.1 JSON) and TLS
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L606](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L606), [server/server.go#L628](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L628), [server/server.go#L647](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L647), [server/server.go#L610](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L610), [server/server.go#L1379](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1379), [server/server.go#L1185](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1185), [server/server.go#L1681](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1681)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: One TCP listener is split with cmux. Without TLS: HTTP/1.x requests (matched by method prefix incl. PATCH) go to the HTTP server; HTTP/2 with `content-type: application/grpc` goes to the gRPC server. With TLS: plain HTTP/1.x on the same port is served by a redirect server that answers `307 Temporary Redirect` to `https://<host>[/<rootpath>]<path>?<query>`; everything else is TLS-terminated (ALPN `http/1.1`,`h2`; certificate taken per-handshake from the settings manager's current server certificate, so rotation needs no restart; certificate sourcing is owned by the TLS/security area), then split again into HTTPS (HTTP/1.1) and gRPC (h2 + grpc content-type). Inside the HTTP server a `handlerSwitcher` routes by exact path first (`/api/badge`, `/auth/logout`), then by `content-type: application/grpc-web+proto` to the grpc-web wrapper (improbable-eng), else to the mux. gRPC-Web therefore works over HTTP/1.1 through proxies.
- **Edge cases**: redirect preserves root path without duplicating it if already present; gRPC over plaintext HTTP/1 is not possible (must use grpc-web); `--insecure` disables TLS entirely (no redirect server); a `TLSConfigCustomizer` (min version/ciphers from flags) is applied to the TLS listener.
- **Configuration surface**: `argocd-server --insecure` / `ARGOCD_SERVER_INSECURE` / `server.insecure`, `--port` (default 8080), `--address`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_LISTEN_ADDRESS`, `--tlsminversion`/`ARGOCD_TLS_MIN_VERSION`, `--tlsmaxversion`/`ARGOCD_TLS_MAX_VERSION`, `--tlsciphers`/`ARGOCD_TLS_CIPHERS` (`server.tls.minversion|maxversion|ciphers` in argocd-cmd-params-cm).
- **Exposure**: API (transport); CLI (`--grpc-web`, `--plaintext`); UI (uses grpc-gateway REST + SSE only).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: same port serves REST JSON, native gRPC (h2) and grpc-web (`application/grpc-web+proto`) calls to `VersionService.Version`.
  - Positive: with TLS enabled, `http://host:port/applications?x=1` returns 307 to `https://host:port/applications?x=1`; with rootpath `/argo` the redirect target is `https://host/argo/...` exactly once.
  - Negative: `--insecure` server rejects TLS handshakes; TLS server rejects h2c gRPC.
  - Recovery: certificate rotation is picked up without restart (settings watch).

### API-003 REST gateway conventions: JSON marshaling, field projection, gzip, content-type (CSRF) enforcement
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1196](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1196), [server/server.go#L1201](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1201), [server/server.go#L1160](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1160), [server/server.go#L1288](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1288), [pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L24), [pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L167](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L167), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L317](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L317), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L318](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L318)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: REST requests under `/api/` are handled by grpc-gateway with a custom JSON marshaler for all MIME types (Kubernetes-style JSON for embedded k8s types, `time.Time` RFC3339). Non-GET requests are rejected with `415 Invalid content type` unless their `Content-Type` (case-insensitive exact match) is in the allow-list (default `application/json`; semicolon-separated; empty list disables enforcement and logs a high-severity CSRF warning). Responses are gzip-compressed when `--enable-gzip` (default true) except when `Accept: text/event-stream`. `GET /api/v1/applications` supports a `fields` query parameter: a comma-separated list of `items.<path>` (and `metadata.resourceVersion`) projecting each Application to a hand-maintained allow-list of paths (`metadata.name|namespace|annotations|labels|creationTimestamp|deletionTimestamp`, `spec`, `spec.destination|project|source|sources|sourceHydrator|syncPolicy`, `status.sourceHydrator|summary|sync.status|sync.revision|health|resources`, `status.operationState.startedAt|finishedAt|phase|operation.sync` (sync projected as empty object presence marker), `operation.sync`); prefix `-` excludes instead of includes. `ProjectService.List` and `ApplicationService.ManagedResources` are overridden to use the argoproj `http.UnaryForwarder`; the UI relies on it to honor `fields=` (`items.metadata.name` for projects, `items.normalizedLiveState,...` for managed resources) — the generic projection lives in the external module `github.com/argoproj/pkg/v2/grpc/http` (see Unconfirmed).
- **Edge cases**: unknown `fields` entries are ignored; the session token returned by `SessionService.Create` is converted into a `Set-Cookie: argocd.token=...` by a forward-response option (see API-005); OpenTelemetry trace context is extracted from incoming HTTP headers.
- **Configuration surface**: `--api-content-types` / `ARGOCD_API_CONTENT_TYPES` / `server.api.content.types` (default `application/json`), `--enable-gzip` / `ARGOCD_SERVER_ENABLE_GZIP` / `server.enable.gzip` (default true).
- **Exposure**: API (REST).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `POST /api/v1/applications` with `Content-Type: application/json` succeeds; `GET ...?fields=items.metadata.name` returns items containing only `metadata.name`.
  - Negative: `POST` with `text/plain` returns 415 `Invalid content type`; with enforcement disabled (empty list) it is accepted.
  - Positive: `Accept-Encoding: gzip` yields gzip body unless `Accept: text/event-stream`.
  - Authorization: field projection never reveals apps the caller cannot `get`.

### API-004 Server-streaming over REST (Server-Sent Events) and log download
- **Upstream**: [pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L126](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L126), [pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L157](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go#L157), [pkg/apiclient/applicationset/forwarder_overwrite.go#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/applicationset/forwarder_overwrite.go#L1), [ui/src/app/shared/services/requests.ts#L69](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/requests.ts#L69), [server/application/application.proto#L367](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L367)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: The four server-streaming RPCs (`ApplicationService.Watch`, `WatchResourceTree`, `PodLogs`, `ApplicationSetService.Watch`) are exposed over REST using argoproj `grpc/http` stream forwarders that the UI consumes with `EventSource` (one JSON message `{"result": <msg>}` per SSE `data:` event). Watch forwarders key events by application/applicationset name. `PodLogs` REST (`/api/v1/applications/{name}/logs` and `/pods/{podName}/logs`) supports `download=true`: response becomes `Content-Type: application/octet-stream`, `Content-Disposition: attachment;filename="<namespace>-<podName>-<container>.log"` (falls back to `log.log` if any part is not a valid resource name), body is raw log lines terminated by `\n`, stopping at the `last=true` entry; on stream error the error text is written to the body.
- **Edge cases**: SSE responses are never gzip-compressed (API-003); client disconnect cancels the server stream; the UI probes with `fetch` after an `EventSource` error to surface the real HTTP status (401 triggers re-login).
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API (REST SSE), UI (lists, tree, logs).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `GET /api/v1/stream/applications` with `Accept: text/event-stream` emits ADDED events for existing apps then MODIFIED on change.
  - Positive: `GET /api/v1/applications/a/logs?podName=p&container=c&namespace=n&download=true` returns attachment named `n-p-c.log`.
  - Negative: invalid names in download produce filename `log.log`.
  - Recovery: dropping the connection and reconnecting with `resourceVersion` resumes without duplicates for list watches.

### API-005 API authentication: token sources, renewal, anonymous access, auth-exempt services
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1537](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1537), [server/server.go#L1643](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1643), [server/server.go#L1122](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1122), [server/server.go#L143](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L143), [server/settings/settings.go#L194](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.go#L194), [server/version/version.go#L76](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/version/version.go#L76), [server/session/session.go#L103](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.go#L103), [common/common.go#L130](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L130)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Every gRPC call (and therefore REST/grpc-web) runs `Authenticate` unless the service implements `AuthFuncOverride`. Token lookup order: gRPC metadata `token`; then `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>` (only if the value parses as a JWT); then cookie `argocd.token` from `grpcgateway-cookie` metadata (split cookies `argocd.token`, `argocd.token-1`, ... are joined). Missing token -> `Unauthenticated: no session information`; invalid -> `Unauthenticated: invalid session: <err>`. When anonymous access is enabled (`users.anonymous.enabled: "true"`) a failed/missing token is replaced by empty claims instead of failing, and the caller receives the permissions of `policy.default` (argocd-rbac-cm). With `--disable-auth` authentication is skipped. Tokens near expiry (Argo-issued) or refreshed OIDC tokens are returned via gRPC header `renew-token`, which the gateway converts to `Set-Cookie: argocd.token=...` (Secure unless `--insecure`, path = basehref). Auth-exempt (claims populated best-effort): `VersionService/*`, `SessionService/*`, `SettingsService/Get` (but `SettingsService/GetPlugins` still requires auth).
- **Edge cases**: `Authorization` header without `Bearer ` prefix or with non-JWT value is ignored and lookup falls through to cookie; OIDC groups may be fetched from userinfo endpoint and cached; expired OIDC token with refresh token is transparently refreshed (cache keyed by sub/sid).
- **Configuration surface**: `users.anonymous.enabled` (argocd-cm), `--disable-auth` / `ARGOCD_SERVER_DISABLE_AUTH`, cookie name `argocd.token`.
- **Exposure**: API, CLI (`--auth-token`, `ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN`, `-H`), UI (cookie).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: same RPC succeeds with metadata token, with Bearer header, and with cookie.
  - Negative: no token -> 401/Unauthenticated `no session information`; garbage Bearer + valid cookie -> cookie used.
  - Positive: anonymous enabled -> unauthenticated call succeeds with exactly the `policy.default` permissions.
  - Positive: token within renewal window yields `Set-Cookie` on REST response.
  - Authorization: `SettingsService/Get` works unauthenticated; `GetPlugins` returns 401 unauthenticated.

### API-006 Minimum client version enforcement (User-Agent)
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L164](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L164), [util/grpc/useragent.go#L26](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/grpc/useragent.go#L26), [common/common.go#L125](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L125), [common/common.go#L365](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L365)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Unary and stream interceptors parse the first `user-agent` metadata value split on whitespace. Tokens of the form `argocd-client/<semver>` (pre-release suffix after `-` stripped) must satisfy `>= 1.4.0`, else `FailedPrecondition: unsatisfied client version constraint: ...`; unparsable version -> `InvalidArgument: could not parse version from user-agent`. Legacy clients (detected by `isLegacyClient`) are rejected. User agents that do not use the `argocd-client` name (browsers, custom clients, grpc-go only) are allowed.
- **Edge cases**: The CLI always sends `argocd-client/<version>`; custom `UserAgent` client option overrides.
- **Configuration surface**: none (constant `MinClientVersion = "1.4.0"`).
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `argocd-client/v3.5.5` and `grpc-go/1.x` accepted.
  - Negative: `argocd-client/v1.3.0` -> FailedPrecondition; `argocd-client/notaversion` -> InvalidArgument.

### API-007 gRPC server limits and options
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L928](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L928), [server/server.go#L930](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L930), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L68](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L68), [common/common.go#L347](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L347), [server/server.go#L167](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L167)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Max send/receive message size = `ARGOCD_GRPC_MAX_SIZE_MB` (default 200) MiB on both server and CLI client; connection timeout 300s; keepalive enforcement policy min time from `ARGOCD_GRPC_KEEP_ALIVE_MIN` (Go duration, default 10s; invalid value logs a warning and uses the default). Login concurrency is limited by `ARGOCD_MAX_CONCURRENT_LOGIN_REQUESTS_COUNT` (default 50; 0 disables) for `SessionService.Create`.
- **Edge cases**: responses larger than the limit fail with `ResourceExhausted`; server comment says 100MB but the effective default is 200MB.
- **Configuration surface**: env `ARGOCD_GRPC_MAX_SIZE_MB`, `ARGOCD_MAX_CONCURRENT_LOGIN_REQUESTS_COUNT`, `ARGOCD_API_SERVER_REPLICAS` (default 1; the login concurrency limit is divided by this value).
- **Exposure**: API, CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: 150 MiB manifest response succeeds with default limit.
  - Negative: with `ARGOCD_GRPC_MAX_SIZE_MB=1`, a >1 MiB response fails with ResourceExhausted.
  - Recovery: concurrent login over limit waits/fails without crashing server.

### API-008 Interceptor chain: audit-safe payload logging, metrics, error translation, panic recovery, tracing
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L937](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L937), [server/server.go#L961](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L961), [server/server.go#L973](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L973), [server/server.go#L181](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L181)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Order (unary): URL-unescape workaround (API-009) -> request logging -> Prometheus server metrics -> authentication -> user-agent check -> payload logging -> Kubernetes error code translation -> Git error code translation -> panic recovery; stream chain is the same without the unescape step. Payload logging is suppressed for sensitive methods: `ClusterService/Create|Update`, `SessionService/Create`, `AccountService/UpdatePassword`, `GPGKeyService/CreateGnuPGPublicKey`, `RepositoryService/Create|Update|CreateRepository|UpdateRepository|ValidateAccess|CreateWriteRepository|UpdateWriteRepository|ValidateWriteAccess`, `RepoCredsService/Create*/Update*` (incl. write variants), `ApplicationService/PatchResource`, `ApplicationService/GetManifestsWithFiles`. Kubernetes `NotFound/AlreadyExists/Forbidden/...` API errors are mapped to matching gRPC codes; panics become `Internal` errors and are logged. gRPC handling-time histogram enabled by `ARGOCD_ENABLE_GRPC_TIME_HISTOGRAM=true`. OpenTelemetry stats handler wraps the server.
- **Edge cases**: the sensitive list names `/gpgkey.GPGKeyService/CreateGnuPGPublicKey` which is not an RPC name (actual is `Create`), so GPG create payloads are logged.
- **Configuration surface**: `ARGOCD_ENABLE_GRPC_TIME_HISTOGRAM`, `--otlp-*` flags.
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `SessionService.Create` request body never appears in logs; `ApplicationService.Get` request is logged.
  - Negative: a handler panic returns `Internal` and the server keeps serving.
  - Positive: k8s NotFound from a handler is returned as gRPC NotFound / HTTP 404.

### API-009 Escaped path-segment handling for repository URLs and cluster IDs
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1696](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1696), [server/server.go#L1717](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1717), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L226](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L226)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Because repository URLs and cluster server URLs are embedded in REST paths, the HTTP handler restores `URL.RawPath` for paths matching `/api/v1/clusters/<x>`, `/api/v1/repositories/<x>[/apps[/<y>]]`, `/api/v1/write-repositories/<x>`, `/api/v1/repocreds/<x>`, `/api/v1/write-repocreds/<x>` and UI route `/settings/clusters/<x>`; then a unary interceptor `url.QueryUnescape`s `RepoQuery.repo`, `RepoAppsQuery.repo`, `RepoAppDetailsQuery.source.repoURL`, `RepoUpdateRequest.repo.repo`, `RepoCredsQuery.url`, `RepoCredsDeleteRequest.url`, `ClusterQuery.id.value`, `ClusterUpdateRequest.id.value`. Cluster IDs additionally support `id.type` = `server` (default), `name`, or `name_escaped` (value is URL-unescaped again).
- **Edge cases**: invalid percent-encoding returns an error before the handler; double-encoded URLs (UI uses `encodeURIComponent`) decode correctly.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API (REST), UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `GET /api/v1/repositories/https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2Fa%2Fb` returns repo `https://github.com/a/b`.
  - Positive: `GET /api/v1/clusters/in-cluster?id.type=name` and `id.type=name_escaped` with an escaped name.
  - Negative: malformed `%zz` returns error.

### API-010 Root path and base href (serving behind a reverse proxy sub-path)
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1143](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1143), [server/server.go#L1433](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1433), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L309](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L309), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L310](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L310), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L25](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L25)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `--rootpath /x` mounts the whole HTTP handler under `/x/` (prefix stripped) while `/healthz` stays also available at root; `--basehref` rewrites `<base href="...">` in `index.html` (computed once) so the SPA, its REST calls (`<base>/api/v1`) and cookies (path) use the sub-path. If both are set and differ a warning is logged (`--basehref and --rootpath had conflict`). CLI uses `--grpc-web-root-path` to reach a server under a sub-path.
- **Edge cases**: basehref `/` or empty leaves index unchanged; swagger spec URL incorporates rootpath; HTTPS redirect includes rootpath.
- **Configuration surface**: `--rootpath`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_ROOTPATH`/`server.rootpath` (default empty), `--basehref`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_BASEHREF`/`server.basehref` (default `/`).
- **Exposure**: API, UI, CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: with rootpath `/argo`, `GET /argo/api/version` works and `GET /api/version` is 404; `/healthz` works at both.
  - Positive: with basehref `/argo/`, index.html contains `<base href="/argo/">`.
  - Positive: CLI with `--grpc-web-root-path argo` succeeds.

### API-011 Health checks, gRPC health service and reflection
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L436](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L436), [util/healthz/healthz.go#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/healthz/healthz.go#L13), [server/server.go#L990](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L990), [server/server.go#L1006](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1006)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `GET /healthz` returns `200 ok` when the server is available, `503` while starting or after a termination signal. `?full=true` additionally lists clusters and fails only on the specific "not an object" decoding error. The standard `grpc.health.v1.Health` service and gRPC server reflection are registered.
- **Edge cases**: health handler is registered both inside the root-path mux and at root.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `/healthz` -> 200 `ok`; `grpc.health.v1.Health/Check` -> SERVING.
  - Negative: during graceful shutdown `/healthz` -> 503.
  - Positive: `grpcurl list` via reflection shows all 13 services.

### API-012 OpenAPI/Swagger documentation endpoints
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1253](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1253), [util/swagger/swagger.go#L15](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/swagger/swagger.go#L15), [util/swagger/swagger.go#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/swagger/swagger.go#L24), [ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L81](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L81)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `GET /swagger.json` serves the embedded aggregated OpenAPI v2 spec (generated from the protos, `assets/swagger.json`); `GET /swagger-ui` serves a ReDoc page loading that spec (spec/script URLs include rootpath). Both responses set `X-Frame-Options: DENY` and `Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors 'none'`. Help page links to `swagger-ui`.
- **Edge cases**: no authentication required for these endpoints.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API, UI (Help page).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `/swagger.json` is valid JSON containing all REST paths of API-001.
  - Positive: anti-framing headers present on both endpoints.

### API-013 Static UI asset serving, SPA fallback and security headers
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L361](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L361), [server/server.go#L1464](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1464), [server/server.go#L1515](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1515), [server/server.go#L149](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L149), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L307](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L307), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L329](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L329), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L330](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L330)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: UI assets are served from the embedded `dist/app` overlaid with an optional directory (`--staticassets`, default `/shared/app`; missing directory is tolerated with a warning). Requests whose `Accept` contains `text/html` and whose path is not an existing file (GET/HEAD) return `index.html` with no-cache headers (HTML5 history routing). `main.<20 hex>.js` bundles get `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` when the file exists, `no-cache` otherwise. Every static response sets `X-Frame-Options` (default `sameorigin`, empty disables), `Content-Security-Policy` (default `frame-ancestors 'self';`, empty disables) and `X-XSS-Protection: 1`. gzip applies if enabled.
- **Edge cases**: `/index.html` requested explicitly is still treated as non-file (served through the HTML path); overlay files take effect for `ui.cssurl` relative paths (API-112).
- **Configuration surface**: `--staticassets`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_STATIC_ASSETS`/`server.staticassets` (default `/shared/app`), `--x-frame-options`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_X_FRAME_OPTIONS`/`server.x.frame.options` (default `sameorigin`), `--content-security-policy`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY` (default `frame-ancestors 'self';`).
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP), UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `GET /applications/foo` with `Accept: text/html` returns index.html (200) with no-cache headers.
  - Positive: overlay file `/shared/app/custom/x.css` is served at `/custom/x.css`.
  - Positive: header defaults present; empty flag values remove the headers.

### API-014 CLI binary download endpoint
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1416](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1416), [ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L52](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L52), [docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L24)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: If an `argocd` binary is found on the server's `PATH`, `GET /download/argocd-linux` and `GET /download/argocd-linux-<GOARCH>` serve it (`http.ServeFile`). If not found, the routes are not registered (warning `argocd not in PATH`). Help page always links to `download/argocd-linux`.
- **Edge cases**: arch-suffixed route is backward-compatible only for the server's own arch.
- **Configuration surface**: `help.download.<os>-<arch>` (extra links, see API-109).
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP), UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: binary present -> both routes 200 with identical bytes.
  - Negative: binary absent -> routes fall through to SPA/static handling.

### API-015 Auxiliary HTTP endpoints inventory (non-gRPC)
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1181](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1181), [server/server.go#L1182](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1182), [server/server.go#L1227](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1227), [server/server.go#L1263](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1263), [server/server.go#L1277](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1277), [server/server.go#L1367](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1367), [common/common.go#L165](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L165)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Besides `/api/v1/**` and `/api/version` (gateway), the HTTP server exposes: `/api/badge` (API-043), `/auth/logout` (OIDC/local logout & token revocation; any method; owned by auth area), `/terminal` (API-044), `/extensions/<name>/**` (API-046, only when proxy extensions enabled), `/extensions.js` (API-050), `/api/webhook` (Git/Helm/OCI webhooks; owned by webhook area), `/api/dex/**`, `/auth/login`, `/auth/callback` (only when SSO configured; owned by SSO area), `/swagger.json`, `/swagger-ui`, `/healthz`, `/download/argocd-linux[-arch]`, `/` static assets.
- **Edge cases**: `/api/badge` and `/auth/logout` are matched by exact path before content-type routing; SSO routes absent when SSO not configured.
- **Configuration surface**: see individual features.
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: each endpoint responds when its feature is enabled.
  - Negative: `/auth/login` returns SPA/404 when SSO is not configured; `/extensions/x` not routed when proxy extensions disabled.

### API-016 AccountService RPC family (accounts, tokens, can-i, password)
- **Upstream**: [server/account/account.go#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L42), [server/account/account.go#L124](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L124), [server/account/account.go#L174](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L174), [server/account/account.go#L188](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L188), [server/account/account.go#L218](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L218), [server/account/account.go#L266](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.go#L266), [server/account/account.proto#L79](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/account/account.proto#L79)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: RPCs: `CanI` (`GET /api/v1/account/can-i/{resource}/{action}/{subresource=**}`) returns `{"value":"yes"|"no"}`; action and resource must be in the RBAC enums (`get, create, update, delete, sync, override, action, invoke` / `clusters, projects, applications, applicationsets, repositories, write-repositories, certificates, accounts, gpgkeys, logs, exec, extensions`), else InvalidArgument `[...] does not contain X`; for project-scoped resources a 3-part `project/ns/name` subresource is normalized (default ns collapses to `project/name`). `UpdatePassword` (`PUT /api/v1/account/password`, body `*`: `name`, `currentPassword`, `newPassword`): empty `name` means self; changing another user's password or any change by an SSO-issued token requires `accounts, update, <name>`; local self-change requires correct `currentPassword` of the *caller* (InvalidArgument `current password does not match`); project tokens cannot change passwords; SSO callers must have a token issued within the last 5 minutes (`ChangePasswordSSOTokenMaxAge`; error `SSO token is too old. Please use 'argocd relogin' to get a new token`); new password must match `passwordPattern` (default `^.{8,32}$`); stores bcrypt hash and `passwordMtime`. `ListAccounts` (`GET /api/v1/account`) returns accounts the caller may `get` (self always). `GetAccount` (`GET /api/v1/account/{name}`). `CreateToken` (`POST /api/v1/account/{name}/token`, `expiresIn` seconds, optional `id`): requires `apiKey` capability (`account '<n>' does not have apiKey capability`), unique id (`account already has token with id`), returns JWT. `DeleteToken` (`DELETE /api/v1/account/{name}/token/{id}`): NotFound `token with id '<id>' does not exist`. Account entries expose `name, enabled, capabilities, tokens[{id, issuedAt, expiresAt}]` sorted by issuedAt desc.
- **Edge cases**: account always has implicit get/update on itself only when issuer is Argo CD (local); `admin` account is a regular entry; disabled accounts still listed.
- **Configuration surface**: `accounts.<name>` (capabilities `login`,`apiKey`), `accounts.<name>.enabled`, `admin.enabled` (argocd-cm); `accounts.<name>.password`, `.tokens` (argocd-secret); `passwordPattern` (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd account *`; UI Settings > Accounts, User Info.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: self password change with correct current password; token create/delete for self without RBAC grant.
  - Negative: wrong current password -> InvalidArgument; password not matching pattern -> error naming the pattern; token for account without `apiKey` -> error.
  - Authorization: user without `accounts, update, bob` cannot change bob's password or tokens; `ListAccounts` hides accounts without `get`.
  - Positive: `can-i get applications default/*` returns yes/no correctly; invalid action -> InvalidArgument.

### API-017 ApplicationService — CRUD family (List, Get with refresh, Create, Update, UpdateSpec, Patch, Delete)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L176](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L176), [server/application/application.go#L288](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L288), [server/application/application.go#L348](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L348), [server/application/application.go#L780](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L780), [server/application/application.go#L1051](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1051), [server/application/application.go#L1068](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1068), [server/application/application.go#L1090](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1090), [server/application/application.go#L1162](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1162), [server/application/application.go#L1321](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1321)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**:
  - Access helper: if a `project` is supplied, RBAC is checked against `<project>/[ns/]name` first (and the app is still fetched to equalize timing); missing app with project -> `NotFound`; without project -> PermissionDenied (`permission denied`) for both missing and forbidden apps; app in a different project than requested -> NotFound. Apps in non-enabled namespaces -> namespace-not-permitted error. Project lookup failures yield `InvalidArgument: app is not allowed in project "<p>", or the project does not exist`.
  - `List` (`GET /api/v1/applications`; query: `name`, `projects`/`project` (repeated, both honored), `selector` (k8s label selector), `repo` (source repoURL filter), `appNamespace`, `resourceVersion`): reads from informer, filters by name/projects/repo/enabled namespace/RBAC get, sorts by name, sets `metadata.resourceVersion` to informer's last sync RV.
  - `Get` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}`; `refresh=normal|hard`, `project`, `appNamespace`, `resourceVersion`): more than one project -> InvalidArgument `multiple projects specified - the get endpoint accepts either zero or one project`; with `refresh` sets the refresh annotation (hard also requests hydration hard refresh and forces repo-server `GetAppDetails` with `noCache`) and blocks until an app event with higher RV *without* the refresh annotation arrives (context deadline -> `application refresh deadline exceeded`). Resource health may be inferred from the cached tree when `status.resourceHealthSource=appTree`.
  - `Create` (`POST /api/v1/applications`, body `application`; query `upsert`, `validate` default true): RBAC `create`; validates name non-empty, unique source names, project permissions, destination, source (when validate); strips any `operation` field; on AlreadyExists returns existing if spec/labels/annotations/finalizers equal, else InvalidArgument `existing application spec is different, use upsert flag to force update`, or with `upsert` requires `update` and updates. Waits up to 2s for informer sync.
  - `Update` (`PUT /api/v1/applications/{application.metadata.name}`, body `application`; `validate`, `project`) and `UpdateSpec` (`PUT /api/v1/applications/{name}/spec`, body `spec`; returns spec): RBAC `update`; moving to another project requires `create` on new and `update` on old rbacName; retries on conflict.
  - `Patch` (`PATCH /api/v1/applications/{name}`, body `{patch, patchType}`): `patchType` `json` or empty = RFC6902 JSON patch, `merge` = RFC7386 merge patch, other -> InvalidArgument `Patch type '<t>' is not supported`; result validated like Update.
  - `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/applications/{name}`; `cascade` (default true), `propagationPolicy` `foreground` (default)|`background`): RBAC `delete`; `cascade=false` + policy -> InvalidArgument `cannot set propagation policy when cascading is disabled`; invalid policy -> InvalidArgument `invalid propagation policy`; cascading adds the matching `resources-finalizer.argocd.argoproj.io[/background]` finalizer (unless already deleting), non-cascade removes it; then deletes the CR; emits audit event.
- **Edge cases**: `Get` with `resourceVersion` reads that RV from API server; Create of an app in a namespace not enabled -> error; Patch on a deleted-in-flight app follows update conflict retries; audit events (`ResourceCreated/Updated/Deleted`) are emitted as k8s Events subject to `--enable-k8s-event`.
- **Configuration surface**: `--application-namespaces`/`application.namespaces`, `--enable-k8s-event`, annotation `argocd.argoproj.io/refresh`.
- **Exposure**: CRD (Application); API; CLI `argocd app create|get|list|set|unset|edit|patch|delete|add-source|remove-source`; UI list/details/create wizard/summary edit.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create, get, list (with selector, projects, repo filter), update, patch (json & merge), delete cascade/non-cascade.
  - Negative: create differing spec without upsert -> InvalidArgument message; patch type `strategic` -> InvalidArgument; Get with two projects -> InvalidArgument.
  - Authorization: get of non-existent app without project returns 403 (not 404); with project returns 404; list omits forbidden apps; project move without `create` in target fails.
  - Recovery: `Get?refresh=hard` returns once controller clears annotation; times out cleanly on client deadline.

### API-018 ApplicationService — Watch (application event stream)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L1226](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1226), [server/application/application.go#L1251](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1251), [server/application/application.proto#L367](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L367)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `Watch` (`GET /api/v1/stream/applications`, server-stream of `ApplicationWatchEvent{type, application}`) accepts the `ApplicationQuery` fields (`name`, `appNamespace`, `projects`, `selector`, `resourceVersion`). If no `resourceVersion` is given, or a single `name` is watched, an initial `ADDED` event is sent for each matching app (sorted by qualified name). Subsequent broadcaster events are sent only if: project matches, app RV >= requested RV, name/namespace match (when given), label selector matches, namespace enabled, and caller has `applications, get`. Health of resources may be inferred from tree before sending.
- **Edge cases**: invalid selector -> error `error parsing labels with selectors`; events for apps the user cannot read are silently dropped; stream ends when client cancels.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API (gRPC stream, SSE); CLI (`app wait`, `app sync` progress via `WatchApplicationWithRetry`); UI (list & details live updates, 500 ms buffering).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: initial ADDED burst then MODIFIED/DELETED events.
  - Positive: `resourceVersion` set + no name -> no initial burst.
  - Authorization: user with access to project A never receives events for project B.
  - Recovery: client reconnect resumes with last seen RV.

### API-019 ApplicationService — operations (Sync, Rollback, TerminateOperation)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L2084](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2084), [server/application/application.go#L2200](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2200), [server/application/application.go#L2265](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2265), [server/application/application.go#L2508](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2508), [server/application/application.proto#L447](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L447)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**:
  - `Sync` (`POST /api/v1/applications/{name}/sync`, body `*`: `revision`, `revisions[]`+`sourcePositions[]` (1-based), `dryRun`, `prune`, `strategy{apply{force}|hook{force}}`, `resources[]` (group/kind/name/namespace), `manifests[]` (local sync), `infos[]`, `retryStrategy`, `syncOptions.items[]`, `appNamespace`, `project`): requires `get` then checks project sync windows (`PermissionDenied: cannot sync: blocked by sync window` / `invalid sync window`), then `sync`. Local `manifests` require `override` and are rejected (`FailedPrecondition`) when automated sync is enabled (unless dryRun) or when source integrity criteria apply. Syncing to a revision different from targetRevision requires `override` only if `application.sync.requireOverridePrivilegeForRevisionSync: "true"`, and is rejected with FailedPrecondition `Cannot sync to X: auto-sync currently set to Y` when automated sync is enabled (non-dry-run). Deleting app -> FailedPrecondition `application is deleting`. `Replace=true` sync option rejected with FailedPrecondition when `--sync-with-replace-allowed=false`. Out-of-range source position -> `source position is out of range`. Request sync options/retry override the app's. Revisions are resolved via repo-server (`NoRevisionCache`), commit SHAs short-circuit for non-Helm. Sets `operation` (initiatedBy username) and emits `OperationStarted` event `initiated [partial ]sync to <rev>` or `initiated sync locally`.
  - `Rollback` (`POST /api/v1/applications/{name}/rollback`, body: `id`, `dryRun`, `prune`): requires `sync`; rejected if deleting, if automated sync enabled (`rollback cannot be initiated when auto-sync is enabled`), if history id missing (InvalidArgument `... does not have deployment with id N`), or history lacks source (`cannot rollback to revision deployed with Argo CD v0.11 or lower`); creates a sync operation with apply strategy and the historical source(s)/revision(s).
  - `TerminateOperation` (`DELETE /api/v1/applications/{name}/operation`): requires `sync`; InvalidArgument `Unable to terminate operation. No operation is in progress`; sets phase `Terminating` with up to 10 conflict retries (100 ms), else Internal `Failed to terminate app. Too many conflicts`.
- **Edge cases**: multi-source apps use `revisions`/`sourcePositions` and ignore `revision`; sync of an app with `spec.sourceHydrator` uses hydrated sources; `syncOptions` empty list vs nil distinguishes override.
- **Configuration surface**: `application.sync.requireOverridePrivilegeForRevisionSync` (argocd-cm), `--sync-with-replace-allowed`/`ARGOCD_SYNC_WITH_REPLACE_ALLOWED` (default true), AppProject `spec.syncWindows`, `spec.sourceIntegrity`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `app sync|rollback|terminate-op|wait`; UI sync panel, history & rollback, Sync Apps bulk panel.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: sync with prune/dryRun/resources/options/retry; rollback to history id; terminate a running op.
  - Negative: sync in deny window -> 403; rollback with autosync -> FailedPrecondition; terminate with no op -> InvalidArgument; Replace when disallowed -> FailedPrecondition.
  - Authorization: user with `get` but not `sync` -> PermissionDenied; local manifests without `override` -> PermissionDenied; revision override with setting enabled and no `override` -> PermissionDenied.
  - Recovery: concurrent terminate/updates resolved via conflict retries.

### API-020 ApplicationService — manifests (GetManifests, GetManifestsWithFiles)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L491](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L491), [server/application/application.go#L652](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L652), [server/application/application.proto#L405](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L405), [server/application/application.proto#L410](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L410)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `GetManifests` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/manifests`; `revision`, `revisions[]`+`sourcePositions[]`, `noCache`, `appNamespace`, `project`) renders target manifests via repo-server using project-permitted Helm/OCI repos & creds, Kustomize/Helm settings, enabled source types and tracking method; returns `repository.ManifestResponse` (manifests as JSON strings, revision, sourceType, ...); for multi-source apps manifests from all sources are concatenated. `GetManifestsWithFiles` is client-streaming: first message `query{name, checksum, appNamespace, project}` then `chunk{bytes}` messages containing a tar.gz of a local directory; the server verifies checksum and renders locally provided files (used by `argocd app diff --local`/`app manifests --local`).
- **Edge cases**: empty name -> `invalid request: application name is missing`; checksum mismatch -> error; payloads are excluded from logs; source types disabled in settings fail generation.
- **Configuration surface**: `kustomize.buildOptions`, `helm.*`, `application.resourceTrackingMethod`, `<type>.enable` generation flags (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `app manifests`, `app diff --local`, `app sync --local`; UI (desired manifests via managed resources).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: manifests at HEAD and at explicit revision; multi-source with positions.
  - Negative: missing name; unauthorized repo for project.
  - Authorization: `applications, get` required.

### API-021 ApplicationService — resource tree, managed resources and server-side diff
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L1638](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1638), [server/application/application.go#L1647](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1647), [server/application/application.go#L1831](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1831), [server/application/application.go#L2992](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2992), [server/application/application.proto#L455](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L455)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ResourceTree` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{applicationName}/resource-tree`) returns the controller-cached `ApplicationTree` (nodes, orphanedNodes, hosts). `WatchResourceTree` (`GET /api/v1/stream/applications/{applicationName}/resource-tree`) streams the tree on every cache change notification. `ManagedResources` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{applicationName}/managed-resources`; filters `namespace,name,version,group,kind`) returns cached `ResourceDiff` items excluding hooks (target/live/normalized/predicted states, diff, modified flag). `ServerSideDiff` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{appName}/server-side-diff`; `liveResources[]`, `targetManifests[]`) performs a server-side-apply dry-run diff against the destination cluster using app diff settings and returns `{items, modified}`. All require `applications, get`.
- **Edge cases**: when the app state is not yet in cache the server may request a refresh and wait (`getCachedAppState`); Secrets are masked in diff states.
- **Configuration surface**: resource customizations / ignoreDifferences (other areas).
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `app get` (tree output), `app resources`, `app diff --server-side-generate`; UI tree/network/list/pods views, DIFF tab.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: tree matches live resources; watch emits on change; managed resources filter by kind.
  - Negative: unknown app -> 403 without project.
  - Authorization: `get` required.

### API-022 ApplicationService — live resource operations (GetResource, PatchResource, DeleteResource)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L1485](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1485), [server/application/application.go#L1520](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1520), [server/application/application.go#L1546](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1546), [server/application/application.go#L1558](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1558), [server/application/application.go#L1601](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1601)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Resource requests identify `name` (app), `namespace`, `resourceName`, `version`, `group`, `kind` (+`appNamespace`, `project`). The resource must be a node (with UID) of the app's tree, else InvalidArgument `<kind> <group> <name> not found as part of application <app>`. `GetResource` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/resource`) returns live manifest JSON; Secret data is masked (`HideSecretData`, sensitive annotations). `PatchResource` (`POST /api/v1/applications/{name}/resource`, body `patch`, query `patchType` e.g. `application/merge-patch+json`) applies the patch; for core Secrets the underlying error is hidden (`failed to patch Secret ns/name`); returns masked manifest; audit event. `DeleteResource` (`DELETE /api/v1/applications/{name}/resource`; `force`, `orphan`): orphan -> propagation Orphan; force -> Background + grace 0; default Foreground; audit event. RBAC: get uses `applications, get`; patch/delete use `update`/`delete` on the app, with fine-grained form `update/<group>/<kind>/<ns>/<name>`: if `server.rbac.disableApplicationFineGrainedRBACInheritance: "true"` only the fine-grained action is checked; otherwise the plain action is tried first and the fine-grained one on PermissionDenied.
- **Edge cases**: the `version` in request overrides node version for Get; cluster credentials honor impersonation when enabled.
- **Configuration surface**: `server.rbac.disableApplicationFineGrainedRBACInheritance` (argocd-cm), `resource.sensitive.mask.annotations`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `app get-resource|patch-resource|delete-resource`; UI resource panel (Live Manifest edit, Delete).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: get/patch/delete a Deployment of the app; Secret values masked.
  - Negative: resource not in tree -> InvalidArgument message.
  - Authorization: fine-grained policy `update/apps/Deployment/ns/x` allows patch only of that resource when inheritance disabled.

### API-023 ApplicationService — resource actions (ListResourceActions, RunResourceAction [deprecated], RunResourceActionV2)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L2561](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2561), [server/application/application.go#L2583](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2583), [server/application/application.go#L2640](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2640), [server/application/application.go#L2662](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2662), [server/application/application.proto#L503](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L503), [resource_customizations/argoproj.io/Application/actions/discovery.lua#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/resource_customizations/argoproj.io/Application/actions/discovery.lua#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA (V1 Deprecated)
- **Semantics**: `ListResourceActions` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/resource/actions`) runs Lua discovery for the live object and returns `[{name, disabled, iconClass, displayName, params}]`. The Application itself can be targeted when group=`argoproj.io`, kind=`Application`, resourceName=name (e.g. built-in `toggle-auto-sync`). `RunResourceActionV2` (`POST .../resource/actions/v2`, body `*` incl. `resourceActionParameters[{name,value}]`) executes the Lua action; resulting patched/created objects are applied (created objects must be permitted by project). RBAC: `applications, action/<group>/<kind>/<action>`. V1 (`POST .../resource/actions`, body = `action` string) logs a deprecation warning and delegates to V2 without parameters.
- **Edge cases**: no discovery script -> empty list; unknown action -> error `error getting Lua resource action`; Lua errors surfaced.
- **Configuration surface**: `resource.customizations.actions.<group_Kind>` (argocd-cm), built-in `resource_customizations/**/actions`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `app actions list|run`; UI resource kebab menu and Toggle Auto-Sync.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `restart` on Deployment; parameterized action via V2; toggle-auto-sync on Application.
  - Negative: unknown action name -> error.
  - Authorization: policy `action/apps/Deployment/restart` required; denied otherwise.

### API-024 ApplicationService — PodLogs streaming
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L1855](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1855), [server/application/application.proto#L533](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L533), [util/settings/settings.go#L955](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L955)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `PodLogs` (server-stream; `GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/pods/{podName}/logs` and `GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/logs`) query: `namespace`, `podName`, `container`, `sinceSeconds`, `sinceTime`, `tailLines`, `follow`, `untilTime` (RFC3339Nano), `filter` (substring; leading `!` inverts), `matchCase`, `kind`, `group`, `resourceName`, `previous`, `appNamespace`, `project`. If `podName` set it implies kind=Pod/resourceName=podName. Pods are selected from the app tree: any Pod node matching filters or whose ancestor (via parentRefs) matches. More than `server.maxPodLogsToRender` (default 10) pods -> InvalidArgument `max pods to view logs are reached. Please provide more granular query`. Streams from all pods are merged by timestamp (100 ms window); each entry `{content, timeStamp, timeStampStr, podName, last}`; a final `last=true` entry is sent when streams end or when an entry exceeds `untilTime`. Pod stream start errors are emitted as a log line. RBAC: `applications, get` and `logs, get`.
- **Edge cases**: invalid `untilTime` -> error; zero matching pods -> stream closes immediately; client cancel ends goroutines.
- **Configuration surface**: `server.maxPodLogsToRender` (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API (gRPC/SSE/download); CLI `app logs`; UI LOGS tab and fullscreen logs route.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: logs for Deployment aggregates its pods; `filter=!healthz` excludes lines; `tailLines`, `previous`, `follow` honored.
  - Negative: 11 pods selected -> InvalidArgument.
  - Authorization: user with app get but no `logs, get` -> PermissionDenied.

### API-025 ApplicationService — revision metadata (RevisionMetadata, RevisionChartDetails, GetOCIMetadata)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L1664](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1664), [server/application/application.go#L1696](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1696), [server/application/application.go#L1726](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L1726), [server/application/application.proto#L390](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L390)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/revisions/{revision}/metadata` (Git commit author/date/tags/message/signature info), `/chartdetails` (Helm chart description/maintainers/home), `/ocimetadata` (OCI image annotations). Query `sourceIndex` and `versionId` select the source for multi-source apps (and historical sources from `status.history`). Require `applications, get`. Metadata is fetched from repo-server using the app's repository credentials.
- **Edge cases**: RevisionMetadata reads the app via client (not informer) to avoid stale history; unknown revision -> repo-server error.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: API; UI (status panel revision metadata, history panel).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: Git revision metadata, Helm chart details, OCI metadata.
  - Negative: invalid sourceIndex/versionId -> error.

### API-026 ApplicationService — resource events and application sync windows
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L899](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L899), [server/application/application.go#L2880](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2880), [server/application/application.proto#L362](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L362), [server/application/application.proto#L385](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L385)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListResourceEvents` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/events`; `resourceNamespace`, `resourceName`, `resourceUID`) returns k8s events of the Application object (control-plane cluster) when no resource is specified, or of the given managed resource (destination cluster) otherwise. `GetApplicationSyncWindows` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/syncwindows`) returns `{activeWindows, assignedWindows, canSync}` computed from project windows matching the app (`kind, schedule, duration, manualSync`).
- **Edge cases**: invalid window definitions -> error `invalid sync windows`; a resourceName/UID not present in the app tree -> InvalidArgument `<name> not found as part of application <app>`; resource events are read from the destination cluster with the app's cluster config.
- **Configuration surface**: AppProject `spec.syncWindows`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI (app get shows windows), UI (EVENTS tab, status panel sync window indicator).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: app events and resource events; windows active/assigned and canSync false inside deny window.
  - Authorization: `applications, get`.

### API-027 ApplicationService — deep link RPCs (ListLinks, ListResourceLinks)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/application.go#L2327](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2327), [server/application/application.go#L2369](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2369), [server/application/application.go#L2403](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2403), [server/application/application.proto#L543](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.proto#L543)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListLinks` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/links`, query `namespace` = app namespace, `project`) evaluates `application.links` with template context `app`/`application` (app object), `cluster` (sanitized destination cluster: server, name, namespaces, shard, project, labels, annotations) and `managedByURL` (from `argocd.argoproj.io/managed-by-url` annotation if a valid http(s) URL, else the instance `url` setting). `ListResourceLinks` (`GET /api/v1/applications/{name}/resource/links`, `ApplicationResourceRequest`) evaluates `resource.links` with `resource` (live object, Secret data masked; or the Application itself when targeted), `application`/`app`, `cluster`, `project` (status stripped). Returns `{items:[{title,url,description,iconClass}]}`. See API-045 for template/condition semantics.
- **Edge cases**: destination not permitted by project -> error `error getting destination cluster`; destination lookup failure -> links evaluated without cluster; per-link evaluation errors are logged and the link skipped.
- **Configuration surface**: `application.links`, `resource.links` (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API; UI (app summary, resource summary, app list/tile links dropdown).
- **Tests required**: see API-045.

### API-028 ApplicationSetService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L79](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L79), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L234](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L234), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L240](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L240), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L286](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L286), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L383](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L383), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L424](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L424), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L447](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L447), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L458](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L458), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L532](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L532), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L550](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L550), [server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L644](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/applicationset/applicationset.go#L644)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: RBAC object is `<spec.template.spec.project>/[<ns>/]<name>` on resource `applicationsets`. `Get` (`GET /api/v1/applicationsets/{name}`, `appsetNamespace`). `List` (`GET /api/v1/applicationsets`; `projects[]`, `selector`, `appsetNamespace`): informer list, enabled namespaces only, RBAC-filtered, project-filtered, sorted by name, RV = informer RV. `Watch` (`GET /api/v1/stream/applicationsets`; `name`, `projects`, `selector`, `appSetNamespace`, `resourceVersion`): same initial-ADDED/permission filtering pattern as application Watch. `Create` (`POST /api/v1/applicationsets`, body `applicationset`; query `upsert`, `dryRun`): templated project (`{{` in `spec.template.spec.project`) -> error `the Argo CD API does not currently support creating ApplicationSets with templated 'project' fields`; invalid/disabled generators rejected; namespace must be enabled; requires `create` and the project must exist (`InvalidArgument: ApplicationSet references project X which does not exist`); `dryRun=true` runs the generators in-process and returns the ApplicationSet with `status.resources` populated from generated apps without persisting; on AlreadyExists identical spec/labels/annotations/finalizers returns existing, otherwise needs `upsert` (`existing ApplicationSet spec is different, use upsert flag to force update`) plus `update`; project change requires `create` in new and `update` in old; labels/annotations are merged on upsert; 10 conflict retries. `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/applicationsets/{name}`). `ResourceTree` (`GET .../{name}/resource-tree`): synthesized tree whose nodes are `status.resources` (generated Applications) with the AppSet as parentRef. `ListResourceEvents` (`GET .../{name}/events`). `Generate` (`POST /api/v1/applicationsets/generate`, body `*` = `{applicationSet}`): same validation and `create` permission; returns `{applications:[...]}` generated using the server namespace for parameter generation; generator errors include captured generator logs in the message.
- **Edge cases**: SCM provider generators honor `--scm-root-ca-path`, allowed SCM providers and `--enable-scm-providers` on the API server; `Delete` reads the appset before RBAC (non-existent appset returns a not-found error rather than 403).
- **Configuration surface**: CRD ApplicationSet; `--application-namespaces`; API-server appset flags (`--enable-scm-providers`, `--allowed-scm-providers`, `--scm-root-ca-path`, git submodule / new globbing flags).
- **Exposure**: CRD; API; CLI `argocd appset *`; UI ApplicationSets pages (Alpha).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create, dry-run create (no object persisted), generate, list by project/selector, get, tree, events, delete.
  - Negative: templated project -> error; unknown project -> InvalidArgument; differing spec without upsert -> InvalidArgument.
  - Authorization: generate/dry-run require `applicationsets, create` on target project; list hides forbidden appsets; watch filters events.

### API-029 CertificateService RPC family (TLS certs and SSH known hosts)
- **Upstream**: [server/certificate/certificate.go#L31](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/certificate/certificate.go#L31), [server/certificate/certificate.proto#L35](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/certificate/certificate.proto#L35)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListCertificates` (`GET /api/v1/certificates`; `hostNamePattern` (file glob), `certType` (`ssh`|`https`), `certSubType`), `CreateCertificate` (`POST /api/v1/certificates`, body `certificates` = `RepositoryCertificateList`, query `upsert`) batch-creates entries in `argocd-tls-certs-cm` / `argocd-ssh-known-hosts-cm` and returns the created list, `DeleteCertificate` (`DELETE /api/v1/certificates` with the same query selector) removes all matching and returns them. RBAC is all-or-nothing on `certificates` with empty object: `get`/`create`/`delete`.
- **Edge cases**: delete with empty selector semantics follow db layer (pattern must match); duplicate entries without upsert -> error from db.
- **Configuration surface**: ConfigMaps `argocd-tls-certs-cm`, `argocd-ssh-known-hosts-cm`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd cert *`; UI Settings > Repository certificates and known hosts.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: add SSH known hosts and TLS PEM; list filtered by type and glob; delete by host.
  - Negative: invalid PEM/known_hosts data rejected; duplicate without upsert rejected.
  - Authorization: each op denied without matching `certificates` permission.

### API-030 ClusterService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/cluster/cluster.go#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L45), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L53](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L53), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L144](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L144), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L211](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L211), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L226](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L226), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L297](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L297), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L351](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L351), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L386](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L386), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L473](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L473), [server/cluster/cluster.go#L485](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.go#L485), [server/cluster/cluster.proto#L44](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/cluster/cluster.proto#L44)
- **Maturity**: GA
