- **Semantics**: RBAC object is `<project>/<server>` (or `<server>` when unscoped) on resource `clusters`. `List` (`GET /api/v1/clusters`; `server`, `name`, `id{type,value}`): filters then RBAC `get`; responses are sanitized (no credentials) and enriched with cached `info` (serverVersion, connectionState, cacheInfo, applicationsCount) plus deprecated top-level `serverVersion`/`connectionState`. `Create` (`POST /api/v1/clusters`, body `cluster`, query `upsert`): RBAC `create`; verifies connectivity via server version; idempotent if equal; differing spec without upsert -> InvalidArgument (spec-is-different message); upsert delegates to Update; caches connection state Successful. `Get` (`GET /api/v1/clusters/{id.value}`, `id.type` = `server`|`name`|`name_escaped`): missing or forbidden -> PermissionDenied. `Update` (`PUT /api/v1/clusters/{id.value}`, body `cluster`, query `updatedFields[]`): requires `update` on existing; when `updatedFields` empty or contains `project` also requires `update` on the new project object; with `updatedFields` only listed paths (`name`, `namespaces`, `config`, `shard`, `clusterResources`, `labels`, `annotations`, `project`) are copied onto the existing cluster; connectivity is re-verified. `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/clusters/{id.value}`): by name deletes every server with that name (each checked for `delete`). `RotateAuth` (`POST /api/v1/clusters/{id.value}/rotate-auth`): bearer-token clusters only (InvalidArgument `Cluster '<s>' does not use bearer token authentication`); creates a new service-account token secret, verifies it, persists, then deletes old secrets. `InvalidateCache` (`POST /api/v1/clusters/{id.value}/invalidate-cache`): RBAC `update`; sets `refreshRequestedAt=now`, returns cluster.
- **Edge cases**: in-cluster `https://kubernetes.default.svc` may be implicit; name lookups match the first cluster with that name for Get but all for Delete/RotateAuth.
- **Configuration surface**: cluster Secrets (`argocd.argoproj.io/secret-type: cluster`).
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd cluster *`; UI Settings > Clusters.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create/list/get/update (partial `updatedFields`)/delete/rotate-auth/invalidate-cache.
  - Negative: unreachable cluster create fails; rotate-auth on exec/cert cluster -> InvalidArgument.
  - Authorization: project-scoped cluster visible only with `clusters, get, <proj>/<server>`; moving cluster to a project without permission -> PermissionDenied.

### API-031 GPGKeyService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L31](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L31), [server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L49](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L49), [server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L72](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L72), [server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L101](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go#L101), [server/gpgkey/gpgkey.proto#L40](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/gpgkey/gpgkey.proto#L40)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `List` (`GET /api/v1/gpgkeys`), `Get` (`GET /api/v1/gpgkeys/{keyID}`; keyID normalized; unknown -> error `no such key: <id>`), `Create` (`POST /api/v1/gpgkeys`, body `publickey{keyData}`): empty data -> `submitted key data is empty`; returns `{created: GnuPGPublicKeyList, skipped: [keyIDs already present]}`. `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/gpgkeys?keyID=`). RBAC `gpgkeys, get|create|delete` with empty object. Keys stored in `argocd-gpg-keys-cm`.
- **Edge cases**: request field `upsert` is accepted but ignored by the server (existing keys are reported as skipped).
- **Configuration surface**: ConfigMap `argocd-gpg-keys-cm`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd gpg *`; UI Settings > GnuPG keys.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: add armored key -> created; re-add -> skipped; get/list/delete.
  - Negative: empty/invalid key data; get unknown id.
  - Authorization: per action.

### API-032 NotificationService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/notification/notification.go#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/notification/notification.go#L24), [server/notification/notification.proto#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/notification/notification.proto#L45)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListTriggers` (`GET /api/v1/notifications/triggers`), `ListServices` (`GET /api/v1/notifications/services`), `ListTemplates` (`GET /api/v1/notifications/templates`) return `{items:[{name}]}` read from the notifications engine config (`argocd-notifications-cm` / `argocd-notifications-secret`). If the config maps are not found an empty list is returned. No RBAC beyond authentication.
- **Edge cases**: in `ListTriggers` a non-NotFound config error is not returned and dereferences a nil API (handler panic recovered as Internal) — services/templates return the error properly.
- **Configuration surface**: `argocd-notifications-cm`, `argocd-notifications-secret`.
- **Exposure**: API; UI (application summary "Notification subscriptions" editor uses services/triggers lists).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: lists reflect configured trigger/service/template names.
  - Negative: missing ConfigMap -> empty lists (200).
  - Authorization: unauthenticated -> 401.

### API-033 ProjectService — CRUD, detailed and global projects
- **Upstream**: [server/project/project.go#L252](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L252), [server/project/project.go#L289](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L289), [server/project/project.go#L305](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L305), [server/project/project.go#L333](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L333), [server/project/project.go#L346](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L346), [server/project/project.go#L360](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L360), [server/project/project.go#L465](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L465), [pkg/apiclient/project/forwarder_overwrite.go#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/project/forwarder_overwrite.go#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: RBAC object is the project name on resource `projects`. `Create` (`POST /api/v1/projects`, body `*` = `{project, upsert}`): missing project -> InvalidArgument `missing payload 'project' in request`; normalizes policies and validates; existing with equal spec returns existing, differing without upsert -> InvalidArgument (spec-is-different), upsert needs `update` and replaces spec. `List` (`GET /api/v1/projects`) RBAC-filtered. `Get` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}`) normalizes JWT token status. `GetDetailedProject` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}/detailed`) returns `{project, globalProjects, repositories (sanitized, project-scoped), clusters (sanitized, project-scoped)}`. `GetGlobalProjects` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}/globalprojects`) returns projects whose spec is inherited via `globalProjects` label selectors in argocd-cm. `Update` (`PUT /api/v1/projects/{project.metadata.name}`, body `*`): requires `update`; for each newly added destination cluster requires `clusters, update, <server>`; for each added source repo requires `repositories, update, <url>`; if any cluster/namespace resource allow/deny list changed, requires `clusters, update` for every destination; rejects updates that would invalidate existing apps (InvalidArgument `as a result of project update N applications source became invalid and M applications destination became invalid`). `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/projects/{name}`): `default` -> InvalidArgument `name 'default' is reserved and cannot be deleted`; referenced by apps -> InvalidArgument `project is referenced by N applications`. Audit events emitted. At startup all projects' JWT token status is normalized.
- **Edge cases**: Update/Delete reference check only lists Applications in the control-plane namespace; project lock serializes concurrent mutations.
- **Configuration surface**: CRD AppProject; `globalProjects` (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: CRD; API; CLI `argocd proj *`; UI Settings > Projects.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create/upsert/get/list/detailed/global/update/delete.
  - Negative: delete default; delete referenced; update removing a destination in use -> InvalidArgument message.
  - Authorization: adding destination without `clusters, update` -> PermissionDenied; adding source repo without `repositories, update` -> PermissionDenied.

### API-034 ProjectService — project role JWT tokens (CreateToken, DeleteToken)
- **Upstream**: [server/project/project.go#L85](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L85), [server/project/project.go#L95](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L95), [server/project/project.go#L204](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L204), [server/project/project.go#L214](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L214), [server/project/project.proto#L81](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.proto#L81)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `CreateToken` (`POST /api/v1/projects/{project}/roles/{role}/token`, body `*`: `description`, `expiresIn` seconds (0 = no expiry), `id`): role must exist (NotFound `project '<p>' does not have role '<r>'`); caller needs `projects, update, <p>` OR be a member of the role's `groups` (by configured scopes); id must be unique per role (InvalidArgument); generated id = UUID; JWT subject `proj:<project>:<role>`; token metadata `{iat, exp, id}` appended to `status.jwtTokensByRole[role]`. `DeleteToken` (`DELETE /api/v1/projects/{project}/roles/{role}/token/{iat}`, query `id`): same authorization; missing role or token returns success (idempotent); retried on conflict.
- **Edge cases**: tokens are identified by `iat` and/or `id`; legacy `spec.roles[].jwtTokens` normalized into status.
- **Configuration surface**: AppProject `spec.roles[].groups`, `status.jwtTokensByRole`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd proj role create-token|delete-token|list-tokens`; UI project role JWT tokens panel.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create token with expiry, use it to call API under role policies, delete by iat and by id.
  - Negative: unknown role -> NotFound; duplicate id -> InvalidArgument.
  - Authorization: group member without project update can create token for that role only.

### API-035 ProjectService — events, sync window state, project links
- **Upstream**: [server/project/project.go#L496](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L496), [server/project/project.go#L526](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L526), [server/project/project.go#L166](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/project/project.go#L166)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListEvents` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}/events`) returns k8s events whose involvedObject is the project. `GetSyncWindowsState` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}/syncwindows`) returns currently active windows (`[]` if none). `ListLinks` (`GET /api/v1/projects/{name}/links`) evaluates `project.links` with only `project` in context (status stripped); unauthorized -> error `unauthorized access to project <name>`.
- **Edge cases**: invalid window schedules -> error.
- **Configuration surface**: `project.links` (argocd-cm), `spec.syncWindows`.
- **Exposure**: API; UI project details (Events tab, Sync Windows, LINKS).
- **Tests required**: positive/negative/authorization on each (requires `projects, get`).

### API-036 RepoCredsService RPC family (credential templates, incl. write credentials)
- **Upstream**: [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L36](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L36), [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L60](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L60), [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L84](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L84), [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L119](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L119), [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L154](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L154), [server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L178](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.go#L178), [server/repocreds/repocreds.proto#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repocreds/repocreds.proto#L42)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: RBAC object is the credential URL prefix on `repositories` (read creds) or `write-repositories` (write creds). `ListRepositoryCredentials` (`GET /api/v1/repocreds`) returns only `{url, username}` per readable template. `ListWriteRepositoryCredentials` (`GET /api/v1/write-repocreds`) iterates read-credential URLs and returns write credentials with a non-empty password. `CreateRepositoryCredentials` (`POST /api/v1/repocreds`, body `creds`, query `upsert`): missing payload / empty URL -> InvalidArgument `must specify URL`; idempotent if equal; otherwise requires `upsert` (which delegates to Update). `UpdateRepositoryCredentials` (`PUT /api/v1/repocreds/{creds.url}`), `DeleteRepositoryCredentials` (`DELETE /api/v1/repocreds/{url}`) and the `write-repocreds` equivalents. Credentials are stored as Secrets labeled `repo-creds` / `repo-write-creds`.
- **Edge cases**: write-credential listing omits SSH-key/GitHub-App-only write creds (no password) and write creds whose URL has no read template; write repocreds RPCs are not gated by the hydrator flag (unlike write repositories).
- **Configuration surface**: Secrets with label `argocd.argoproj.io/secret-type: repo-creds` / `repo-write-creds`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd repocreds *` (read creds only); UI Settings > Repositories (credential templates, "Save as write credential").
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create/list/update/delete template; URL-encoded path in update/delete.
  - Negative: empty URL; differing spec without upsert.
  - Authorization: list filtered; create denied without `repositories, create, <url>`.

### API-037 RepositoryService — repository CRUD and connection validation (incl. deprecated aliases)
- **Upstream**: [server/repository/repository.go#L87](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L87), [server/repository/repository.go#L97](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L97), [server/repository/repository.go#L137](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L137), [server/repository/repository.go#L185](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L185), [server/repository/repository.go#L218](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L218), [server/repository/repository.go#L431](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L431), [server/repository/repository.go#L436](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L436), [server/repository/repository.go#L547](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L547), [server/repository/repository.go#L604](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L604), [server/repository/repository.go#L683](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L683), [server/repository/repository.proto#L128](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.proto#L128)
- **Maturity**: GA (aliases `List`, `Create`, `Update`, `Delete` Deprecated)
- **Semantics**: RBAC object is `[<project>/]<repoURL>` on `repositories`. `ListRepositories` (`GET /api/v1/repositories`, `forceRefresh`) returns normalized+sanitized repos (no secrets) with `connectionState` (cached per repo+project unless `forceRefresh`; credential configuration errors are reported as `Configuration error - please check the server logs`), sorted by `project/repo`. `Get` (`GET /api/v1/repositories/{repo}`, `appProject`, `forceRefresh`): RBAC `get`; NotFound `repo '<url>' not found` when not configured (credential-template matches are not "configured"). `CreateRepository` (`POST /api/v1/repositories`, body `repo`, query `upsert`, `credsOnly`): RBAC `create`; for non-project-scoped repos, inherits credentials from a matching template if none supplied and tests connectivity before saving; idempotent if equal; differing without upsert -> InvalidArgument (spec-is-different); returns only `{repo, type, name}`. `UpdateRepository` (`PUT /api/v1/repositories/{repo.repo}`): requires `update` on both existing and new project object. `DeleteRepository` (`DELETE /api/v1/repositories/{repo}`, `appProject`, `forceRefresh`): RBAC `delete`; also clears repo caches. `ValidateAccess` (`POST /api/v1/repositories/{repo}/validate`, body `repo`, query: all `RepoAccessQuery` credential fields incl. username/password, sshPrivateKey, tls client cert/key, type `git|helm|oci`, name, enableOci, GitHub App id/installation/private key/enterprise URL, proxy, project, gcpServiceAccountKey, forceHttpBasicAuth, useAzureWorkloadIdentity, bearerToken, insecureOciForceHttp, Azure service principal fields): RBAC `create`; returns empty on success or the connection error.
- **Edge cases**: deprecated aliases share REST bindings with their replacements; repo lookup failures are reported as PermissionDenied to avoid disclosure; project-scoped repos skip pre-save connection test.
- **Configuration surface**: Secrets with `argocd.argoproj.io/secret-type: repository`, legacy `repositories` key (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd repo add|get|list|rm`; UI Settings > Repositories (connect via SSH/HTTPS/GitHub App/Google Cloud/Azure).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: create (each auth type), list with connection state, get, update, delete, validate.
  - Negative: bad credentials -> create fails with connection error; differing without upsert.
  - Authorization: project-scoped repo requires `repositories, create, <proj>/<url>`; list hides unreadable repos.
  - Recovery: `forceRefresh=true` re-tests connection and updates cache.

### API-038 RepositoryService — repository discovery (ListRefs, ListOCITags, ListApps, GetAppDetails, GetHelmCharts)
- **Upstream**: [server/repository/repository.go#L241](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L241), [server/repository/repository.go#L262](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L262), [server/repository/repository.go#L285](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L285), [server/repository/repository.go#L333](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L333), [server/repository/repository.go#L412](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L412), [server/repository/repository.proto#L153](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.proto#L153)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `ListRefs` (`GET /api/v1/repositories/{repo}/refs`) returns `{branches, tags}`; `ListOCITags` (`GET .../{repo}/oci-tags`) returns tags; both RBAC `repositories, get`. `ListApps` (`GET .../{repo}/apps`; `revision`, `appName`, `appProject`): also requires `applications, create` or `update` on `<appProject>/<appName>` (PermissionDenied otherwise) and that the repo is permitted in the project; returns `[{type, path}]` discovered app directories. `GetAppDetails` (`POST /api/v1/repositories/{source.repoURL}/appdetails`, body `*`: `source`, `appName`, `appProject`, `sourceIndex`, `versionId`): missing source -> InvalidArgument; requires repo `get`, `applications, get` on `<appProject>/<appName>`, and `applications, create` when the app does not exist; for existing apps the project must match; returns tool-specific details (Helm values/parameters/valueFiles, Kustomize images, Directory, Plugin parameters). `GetHelmCharts` (`GET .../{repo}/helmcharts`) returns `[{name, versions}]` from Helm repo index.
- **Edge cases**: repo lookups that fail return PermissionDenied; ListApps clones and runs discovery — hence app-level permission gate.
- **Configuration surface**: none beyond repositories.
- **Exposure**: API; UI (create wizard revision/path/chart autocompletion, parameters tab); CLI (indirect).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: refs for git repo; tags for OCI; apps list for a path; app details for helm/kustomize; helm charts list.
  - Authorization: ListApps denied without app create/update; GetAppDetails for existing app in another project rejected.

### API-039 RepositoryService — write repositories (hydrator-gated)
- **Upstream**: [server/repository/repository.go#L159](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L159), [server/repository/repository.go#L199](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L199), [server/repository/repository.go#L495](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L495), [server/repository/repository.go#L570](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L570), [server/repository/repository.go#L624](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L624), [server/repository/repository.go#L734](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/repository/repository.go#L734), [cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L336](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd-server/commands/argocd_server.go#L336)
- **Maturity**: Beta (part of Source Hydrator)
- **Semantics**: `GetWrite` (`GET /api/v1/write-repositories/{repo}`), `ListWriteRepositories` (`GET /api/v1/write-repositories`), `CreateWriteRepository` (`POST /api/v1/write-repositories`), `UpdateWriteRepository` (`PUT /api/v1/write-repositories/{repo.repo}`), `DeleteWriteRepository` (`DELETE /api/v1/write-repositories/{repo}`), `ValidateWriteAccess` (`POST /api/v1/write-repositories/{repo}/validate`) mirror API-037 on resource `write-repositories` (Secrets labeled `repository-write`). All return `Unimplemented: hydrator is disabled` unless the API server runs with `--hydrator-enabled`.
- **Edge cases**: `GetWrite` NotFound message `write repo '<url>' not found`.
- **Configuration surface**: `--hydrator-enabled`/`ARGOCD_HYDRATOR_ENABLED`/`hydrator.enabled` (argocd-cmd-params-cm).
- **Exposure**: API; UI (repo connect "Save as write credential"); no CLI command.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: with hydrator enabled, CRUD + validate write repos.
  - Negative: hydrator disabled -> 501/Unimplemented for all six.
  - Authorization: `write-repositories` permissions distinct from `repositories`.

### API-040 SessionService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/session/session.go#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.go#L45), [server/session/session.go#L58](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.go#L58), [server/session/session.go#L95](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.go#L95), [server/session/session.go#L109](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.go#L109), [server/session/session.proto#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/session/session.proto#L42)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `Create` (`POST /api/v1/session`, body `*`: `username`, `password`, `token`): non-empty legacy `token` -> Unauthenticated `token-based session creation no longer supported. please upgrade argocd cli to v0.7+`; missing creds -> Unauthenticated `no credentials supplied`; verifies local user password (with login rate limit and failure counters), issues a JWT subject `<username>:login` with expiry `users.session.duration` (default 24h) and returns `{token}`; REST response also sets the `argocd.token` cookie. Login success/failure counters exported as metrics. `Delete` (`DELETE /api/v1/session`) returns `{token:""}` (cookie clearing for web). `GetUserInfo` (`GET /api/v1/session/userinfo`) returns `{loggedIn, username, iss, groups}` (groups filtered by configured OIDC scopes). All three are auth-exempt.
- **Edge cases**: disabled account or account without `login` capability fails verification; repeated failures trigger lockout per session manager (auth area).
- **Configuration surface**: `users.session.duration` (argocd-cm), `ARGOCD_MAX_CONCURRENT_LOGIN_REQUESTS_COUNT`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd login` (password), `argocd account get-user-info`; UI login page and user info.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: login returns JWT and cookie; userinfo reflects claims.
  - Negative: wrong password -> Unauthenticated; empty creds -> `no credentials supplied`; legacy token -> message above.
  - Recovery: burst logins beyond concurrency limit do not crash server.

### API-041 SettingsService RPC family
- **Upstream**: [server/settings/settings.go#L41](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.go#L41), [server/settings/settings.go#L128](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.go#L128), [server/settings/settings.go#L163](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.go#L163), [server/settings/settings.proto#L18](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/settings/settings.proto#L18)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `Get` (`GET /api/v1/settings`, auth-exempt) returns: `url`, `additionalUrls`, `appLabelKey`, `statusBadgeEnabled`, `statusBadgeRootUrl`, `kustomizeOptions.buildOptions`, `googleAnalytics{trackingID, anonymizeUsers}`, `help{chatUrl, chatText, binaryUrls}`, `userLoginsDisabled` (true unless some enabled account has `login` capability), `kustomizeVersions`, `uiCssURL`, `trackingMethod`, `installationID`, `execEnabled`, `appsInAnyNamespaceEnabled`, `impersonationEnabled`, `hydratorEnabled`, `syncWithReplaceAllowed`, `dexConfig.connectors[{name,type}]` (parsed from `dex.config`), `oidcConfig{name, issuer, clientID, cliClientID, scopes, idTokenClaims, enablePKCEAuthentication}`. Only when logged in (or auth disabled): `uiBannerContent`, `uiBannerURL`, `uiBannerPermanent`, `uiBannerPosition`, `controllerNamespace`, `resourceOverrides`. Only when logged in: `passwordPattern`. `configManagementPlugins` field is deprecated and not populated. `GetPlugins` (`GET /api/v1/settings/plugins`, authenticated) returns sidecar CMP plugin names from repo-server.
- **Edge cases**: invalid `dex.config` YAML -> `dexConfig` omitted; repo-server unavailable -> GetPlugins error `failed to list sidecar plugins from reposerver`.
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm keys listed above; `--hydrator-enabled`, `--sync-with-replace-allowed`, `--application-namespaces`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI (`login --sso`, relogin read OIDC config); UI (bootstrapping, banner, badge, help, exec, appearance).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: anonymous `GET /api/v1/settings` omits banner/resourceOverrides/passwordPattern; logged-in includes them.
  - Positive: `userLoginsDisabled` true when admin disabled and no login accounts.
  - Negative: `GetPlugins` without auth -> 401.

### API-042 VersionService
- **Upstream**: [server/version/version.go#L30](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/version/version.go#L30), [server/version/version.go#L76](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/version/version.go#L76), [server/version/version.proto#L32](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/version/version.proto#L32)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `Version` (`GET /api/version`, input `google.protobuf.Empty`, auth-exempt): if caller not logged in and neither anonymous access nor `--disable-auth` is on, returns only `{Version}`; otherwise returns `Version, BuildDate, GitCommit, GitTag, GitTreeState, GoVersion, Compiler, Platform, KustomizeVersion, HelmVersion, JsonnetVersion, KubectlVersion, ExtraBuildInfo` (tool versions computed lazily and cached; tool errors are returned as the version string).
- **Edge cases**: UI uses presence of Kustomize/Helm versions while unauthenticated to infer that auth is disabled.
- **Configuration surface**: `users.anonymous.enabled`, `--disable-auth`.
- **Exposure**: API; CLI `argocd version`; UI version panel, help page arch label.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: unauthenticated -> only Version; authenticated -> full message.
  - Positive: anonymous enabled -> full message unauthenticated.

### API-043 Status badge endpoint (`/api/badge`)
- **Upstream**: [server/badge/badge.go#L93](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/badge/badge.go#L93), [server/badge/color.go#L20](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/badge/color.go#L20), [server/server.go#L1181](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1181), [docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L3](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L3), [docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L23](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L23)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Unauthenticated SVG badge. If `statusbadge.enabled` != `"true"` every request returns the default badge `Unknown`/`Unknown` (no app lookup). Query parameters: `namespace` (must be a valid namespace name, else 400; must be an enabled app namespace, else "Not Found" badge), `name` (valid app name, else 400; app not found -> left text `Not Found`, empty right text), `project` (repeatable; each must be a DNS label (case-insensitive) else 400; aggregates all apps of those projects in the namespace: any non-Synced -> `OutOfSync`, any non-Healthy -> `Degraded`, otherwise `Synced`/`Healthy` if at least one app), `revision=true` (shows `(rev)` from `status.operationState.syncResult.revisions[0]` or `.revision`, truncated to 7 chars unless `keepFullRevision=true`; widens to 192/400 px), `width=<int>` (overrides computed width), `showAppName=true` (adds a title row with the app name; height 40). Left color by health (Healthy green #0b612a, Progressing blue #103d66, Degraded red #a72e26, Suspended grey #29343D, Missing/Unknown purple #731f4d), right color by sync (Synced green, OutOfSync orange #bd7300, Unknown purple), else grey. Response headers: `Content-Type: image/svg+xml`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`.
- **Edge cases**: `name` and `project` can be combined (project aggregation overrides single-app status); non-integer width ignored; revision shown only when an operation sync result exists.
- **Configuration surface**: `statusbadge.enabled`, `statusbadge.url` (UI root override) in argocd-cm.
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP); UI badge panel (API-114).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: enabled + healthy synced app -> green/green SVG with texts `Healthy`/`Synced`.
  - Positive: `revision=true` truncation and `keepFullRevision=true`; `showAppName=true` layout; `project=a&project=b` aggregation.
  - Negative: disabled -> `Unknown` badge even for existing app; invalid name -> 400; unknown app -> `Not Found`.
  - Authorization: no authentication required; disabled namespace yields `Not Found`.

### API-044 Web terminal websocket endpoint (`/terminal`)
- **Upstream**: [server/application/terminal.go#L78](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/terminal.go#L78), [server/application/terminal.go#L94](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/terminal.go#L94), [server/application/websocket.go#L135](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/websocket.go#L135), [server/server.go#L1227](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1227), [docs/operator-manual/web_based_terminal.md#L16](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/web_based_terminal.md#L16), [util/settings/settings.go#L1681](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1681)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Authenticated (cookie/bearer) websocket at `/terminal?pod=&container=&appName=&appNamespace=&projectName=&namespace=&shell=`. Returns 404 when `exec.enabled` != `"true"`. Required params missing -> 400 `Missing required parameters`; each name validated (400 `Pod name is not valid`, etc.); app namespace must be enabled (403). RBAC: `applications, get` and `exec, create` on the app rbacName (401 with message on failure). App not found -> 404; project mismatch -> 400 `The wrong project was specified for the app`; pod must belong to the app tree (400 `Pod doesn't belong to specified app`) and container must be running. Shell: requested `shell` if in `exec.shells` allow-list, otherwise each configured shell in order (default `bash,sh,powershell,cmd`) until one starts. Messages are JSON `{operation: "stdin"|"resize"|"stdout", data, rows, cols}`; unknown operation ends the session. Permissions are re-validated during the session and on token refresh the server sends a reconnect message/code; periodic websocket pings keep the connection alive.
- **Edge cases**: with `--disable-auth` validation is skipped; uses impersonation config when enabled.
- **Configuration surface**: `exec.enabled`, `exec.shells` (argocd-cm); RBAC resource `exec` action `create`.
- **Exposure**: API (websocket); UI Terminal tab (API-097).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: exec into running container with default shell fallback; resize messages honored.
  - Negative: exec disabled -> 404; pod not in app -> 400; container not running -> 400.
  - Authorization: user lacking `exec, create` -> 401; permission revoked mid-session -> session ends with "Permission denied".

### API-045 Deep links configuration and evaluation (`application.links`, `resource.links`, `project.links`)
- **Upstream**: [server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L18](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L18), [server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L53](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L53), [server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L76](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L76), [server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L103](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L103), [util/settings/settings.go#L410](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L410), [util/settings/settings.go#L962](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L962), [docs/operator-manual/deep_links.md#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/deep_links.md#L13), [docs/operator-manual/deep_links.md#L47](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/deep_links.md#L47)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Each argocd-cm key holds a YAML list of `{url, title, description?, icon.class?, if?}`. For each link: if `if` is set it is evaluated with expr-lang against the context map; non-boolean result or evaluation error skips the link (error logged); false skips. `url` is rendered with Go `text/template` + Sprig generic functions (with `env`, `expandenv`, `getHostByName` removed); template errors skip the link. Context keys: `resource` (live object; Secret data masked), `application` and alias `app`, `cluster` (only `server, name, namespaces, shard, project, labels, annotations`), `project` (AppProject with status removed), `managedByURL`. Availability: `resource.links` -> resource, app, cluster, project; `application.links` -> app, cluster, managedByURL; `project.links` -> project. Response items `{title, url, description, iconClass}` in config order. UI renders links in application summary ("LINKS"), resource summary, project details, and app list/tile link menus.
- **Edge cases**: invalid YAML -> RPC error `failed to read application deep links from configmap`; missing keys -> empty list; Go templates referencing missing keys render `<no value>`.
- **Configuration surface**: `application.links`, `resource.links`, `project.links` (argocd-cm).
- **Exposure**: API (ListLinks RPCs, API-027/035); UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: link with condition true/false; Sprig function in URL; `index .resource.metadata.labels "a/b"` access.
  - Negative: non-boolean `if` skipped; `{{ env "HOME" }}` fails to parse (function removed); Secret data not available in templates.
  - Authorization: links require `applications, get` / `projects, get`.

### API-046 Proxy extensions — enablement and routing (`/extensions/<name>/...`)
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1229](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1229), [server/server.go#L1305](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1305), [server/extension/extension.go#L32](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L32), [server/extension/extension.go#L760](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L760), [docs/operator-manual/feature-maturity.md#L21](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/feature-maturity.md#L21), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L21](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L21)
- **Maturity**: Beta
- **Semantics**: Disabled by default. When `--enable-proxy-extension` is set the server registers `/extensions/` behind the session auth middleware (unauthenticated -> 401 before extension logic). Path `/extensions/<extName>/<rest>`: missing name -> 400 `Invalid URL: extension name must be provided`; CR/LF stripped from the name. Unregistered extension -> 404 `Extension not found`. The `/extensions/<extName>` prefix is stripped and the remainder is forwarded to the selected backend. If registration fails (invalid config), an error is logged and no extension is routable but the server keeps running.
- **Edge cases**: request method/body/query pass through unchanged; when disabled `/extensions/x` falls through to static handling.
- **Configuration surface**: `--enable-proxy-extension` / `ARGOCD_SERVER_ENABLE_PROXY_EXTENSION` / `server.enable.proxy.extension`.
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP reverse proxy); UI extensions call it.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: enabled + configured extension -> request proxied with prefix stripped.
  - Negative: disabled -> not routed; unknown name -> 404; no token -> 401.

### API-047 Proxy extensions — configuration (`extension.config`, `extension.config.<name>`)
- **Upstream**: [server/extension/extension.go#L149](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L149), [server/extension/extension.go#L213](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L213), [server/extension/extension.go#L429](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L429), [server/extension/extension.go#L476](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L476), [server/extension/extension.go#L562](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L562), [server/extension/extension.go#L579](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L579), [server/extension/extension.go#L599](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L599), [util/settings/settings.go#L1696](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1696), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L43](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L43)
- **Maturity**: Beta
- **Semantics**: Config is read from argocd-cm key `extension.config` (YAML `extensions: [{name, backend}]`) and from any `extension.config.<name>` key (YAML `BackendConfig` for that name). `$`-prefixed values are replaced with argocd-secret keys before parsing. Backend: `connectionTimeout` (default 2s), `keepAlive` (15s), `idleConnectionTimeout` (60s), `maxIdleConnections` (30), `services: [{url, cluster{name, server}?, headers:[{name, value}]?}]`. Validation (any failure rejects the whole configuration): name required and `^[A-Za-z0-9-_]+$`; names unique (`duplicated extension found in the configs for "<n>"`); at least one service; each `url` required; `cluster` required when >1 service; cluster needs name or server; header name and value required; duplicated proxy keys rejected. Config changes are applied without restart by rebuilding the registry.
- **Edge cases**: same extension defined in both `extension.config` and `extension.config.<name>` -> duplicate error; empty config -> "No extensions configured".
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `extension.config`, `extension.config.<name>`; argocd-secret keys referenced by `$key`.
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: both config styles; secret reference in header value.
  - Negative: invalid name chars; two services without cluster; missing url -> no extensions registered.
  - Recovery: editing argocd-cm updates routing without restart.

### API-048 Proxy extensions — request authorization and header contract
- **Upstream**: [server/extension/extension.go#L109](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L109), [server/extension/extension.go#L680](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L680), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L248](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L248), [docs/operator-manual/rbac.md#L269](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/rbac.md#L269)
- **Maturity**: Beta
- **Semantics**: Each request must carry `Argocd-Application-Name: <namespace>:<app-name>` and `Argocd-Project-Name: <project>` (400 `Invalid headers: ...` if missing/malformed/invalid names). Authorization (any failure -> 401 `Unauthorized extension request`): `applications, get` on `RBACName(controlPlaneNs, project, appNs, app)`; `extensions, invoke, <extName>`; application must exist; its project must equal the header project; project must exist and permit the app's destination cluster/namespace.
- **Edge cases**: header-supplied namespace that equals the control plane collapses to `project/name` RBAC form; apps in other namespaces use 3-part names.
- **Configuration surface**: RBAC `p, <sub>, extensions, invoke, <name>, allow`.
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: user with app get + extension invoke succeeds.
  - Negative: missing header -> 400; project mismatch -> 401; destination not permitted -> 401.
  - Authorization: without `extensions, invoke` -> 401 even with app access.

### API-049 Proxy extensions — forwarding, backend selection, outgoing headers and metrics
- **Upstream**: [server/extension/extension.go#L524](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L524), [server/extension/extension.go#L548](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L548), [server/extension/extension.go#L627](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L627), [server/extension/extension.go#L733](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L733), [server/extension/extension.go#L837](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L837), [server/extension/extension.go#L822](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/extension/extension.go#L822), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L294](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/proxy-extensions.md#L294)
- **Maturity**: Beta
- **Semantics**: Single-service extensions always use that service. Multi-service extensions select by app destination: registry keys `(ext, name, "")`, `(ext, "", server)` and `(ext, name, server)`; lookup tries `(ext, dest.name, dest.server)`; no match -> 400 `invalid extension`. Outgoing request: `Authorization` and `Cookie` removed; `Host` set to backend host; configured service headers set (override same-name headers); added headers `Argocd-Namespace` (control plane ns), `Argocd-Target-Cluster-Name` (if dest.name), `Argocd-Target-Cluster-URL` (if dest.server), `Argocd-User-Id` (sub), `Argocd-Username`, `Argocd-User-Groups` (comma-joined). Transport: dial timeout/keepalive/idle timeout/max idle conns per config, TLS handshake 10s, expect-continue 1s. Per-extension request counter by status code and duration histogram are recorded on the API server metrics registry.
- **Edge cases**: configured headers can overwrite `Argocd-*` headers set by the server (they are applied later in the proxy Director).
- **Configuration surface**: see API-047.
- **Exposure**: API.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: backend receives prefix-stripped path, no Authorization/Cookie, expected `Argocd-*` headers, configured header from secret.
  - Positive: two clusters routed to different backends based on app destination.
  - Negative: app destination matching no service -> 400 `invalid extension`.

### API-050 UI extension bundle endpoint (`/extensions.js`)
- **Upstream**: [server/server.go#L1269](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1269), [server/server.go#L1277](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1277), [server/server.go#L1320](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1320), [server/server.go#L1322](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1322), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L4](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L4)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `GET /extensions.js` (`Content-Type: application/javascript`) walks `/tmp/extensions/` recursively and concatenates every regular non-symlink file whose base name matches `^extension(.*)\.js$`, each wrapped as `// source: <path>/<name>` + `try { <content> } catch(e) { console.error('Extension <name> failed to load:', e); }`. Missing directory -> empty 200 response; other walk errors -> 500 `Internal error`. gzip applies if enabled. The UI loads it at startup so extensions can call `window.extensionsAPI`.
- **Edge cases**: symlinked files are skipped; one failing extension does not break others.
- **Configuration surface**: files mounted into `/tmp/extensions` of argocd-server (e.g. via argocd-extension-installer init container).
- **Exposure**: API (HTTP); UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: two extension files concatenated in walk order with wrappers.
  - Negative: file `foo.js` (no `extension` prefix) ignored; symlink ignored; no directory -> empty body.

## Part B — `argocd` CLI

Common CLI behavior applying to every command below: flags may also come from `ARGOCD_OPTS` (API-051); generic failures exit with code **20** (`errors.CheckError`), usage errors/explicit failures with **1**; `-o/--output` formats are command specific (typically `json|yaml|wide`, plus `name`, `tree`, `tree=detailed` where listed); commands registered through `initialize.InitCommand` (app, appset, cluster, repo, repocreds, proj, account, cert, gpg, configure, version) also accept kubectl client flags (`--kubeconfig`, `--context`, `--cluster`, `--user`, `-n/--namespace`, `--token`, `--username`, `--password`, `--proxy-url`, `--request-timeout`, `--insecure-skip-tls-verify`) used only in `--core` mode. The `Doc` column links each generated reference page; tables below are complete for all 170 command pages.

### API-051 CLI global flags, `ARGOCD_OPTS` and environment variables
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L74](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L74), [cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L90](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L90), [util/config/env.go#L23](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/config/env.go#L23), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L208](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L208), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L230](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L230), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Persistent flags on the root command (inherited by all subcommands). Every flag default can be supplied via `ARGOCD_OPTS` (shell-quoted `--flag value` / `--flag` / `--flag=value`; a token not starting with `--` without a pending key -> fatal `ARGOCD_OPTS invalid at '<x>'`). Precedence for server address: CLI `--server` > `ARGOCD_SERVER` env > current context in local config; for token: `--auth-token` (trimmed) > `ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN` > local config user token. `--server-name`, `--controller-name`, `--redis-haproxy-name`, `--redis-name`, `--repo-server-name` default from `ARGOCD_SERVER_NAME`, `ARGOCD_APPLICATION_CONTROLLER_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REDIS_HAPROXY_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REDIS_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REPO_SERVER_NAME`; `--redis-compress` from `REDIS_COMPRESSION` (default `gzip`). `--argocd-context` selects a named context; `--kube-context` overrides the kubeconfig context for core/port-forward modes. Missing server -> error `Argo CD server address unspecified`.

| Flag | Description (default) |
|---|---|
| `--argocd-context string` | The name of the Argo-CD server context to use |
| `--auth-token string` | Authentication token; set this or the ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN environment variable |
| `--client-crt string` | Client certificate file |
| `--client-crt-key string` | Client certificate key file |
| `--config string` | Path to Argo CD config (default "/home/user/.config/argocd/config") |
| `--controller-name string` | Name of the Argo CD Application controller; set this or the ARGOCD_APPLICATION_CONTROLLER_NAME environment variable when the controller's name label differs from the default, for example when installing via the Helm chart (default "argocd-application-controller") |
| `--core` | If set to true then CLI talks directly to Kubernetes instead of talking to Argo CD API server |
| `--grpc-web` | Enables gRPC-web protocol. Useful if Argo CD server is behind proxy which does not support HTTP2. |
| `--grpc-web-root-path string` | Enables gRPC-web protocol. Useful if Argo CD server is behind proxy which does not support HTTP2. Set web root. |
| `-H/--header strings` | Sets additional header to all requests made by Argo CD CLI. (Can be repeated multiple times to add multiple headers, also supports comma separated headers) |
| `--http-retry-max int` | Maximum number of retries to establish http connection to Argo CD server |
| `--insecure` | Skip server certificate and domain verification |
| `--kube-context string` | Directs the command to the given kube-context |
| `--logformat string` | Set the logging format. One of: json\|text (default "json") |
| `--loglevel string` | Set the logging level. One of: debug\|info\|warn\|error (default "info") |
| `--plaintext` | Disable TLS |
| `--port-forward` | Connect to a random argocd-server port using port forwarding |
| `--port-forward-namespace string` | Namespace name which should be used for port forwarding |
| `--prompts-enabled` | Force optional interactive prompts to be enabled or disabled, overriding local configuration. If not specified, the local configuration value will be used, which is false by default. |
| `--redis-compress string` | Enable this if the application controller is configured with redis compression enabled. (possible values: gzip, none) (default "gzip") |
| `--redis-haproxy-name string` | Name of the Redis HA Proxy; set this or the ARGOCD_REDIS_HAPROXY_NAME environment variable when the HA Proxy's name label differs from the default, for example when installing via the Helm chart (default "argocd-redis-ha-haproxy") |
| `--redis-name string` | Name of the Redis deployment; set this or the ARGOCD_REDIS_NAME environment variable when the Redis's name label differs from the default, for example when installing via the Helm chart (default "argocd-redis") |
| `--repo-server-name string` | Name of the Argo CD Repo server; set this or the ARGOCD_REPO_SERVER_NAME environment variable when the server's name label differs from the default, for example when installing via the Helm chart (default "argocd-repo-server") |
| `--server string` | Argo CD server address |
| `--server-crt string` | Server certificate file |
| `--server-name string` | Name of the Argo CD API server; set this or the ARGOCD_SERVER_NAME environment variable when the server's name label differs from the default, for example when installing via the Helm chart (default "argocd-server") |

- **Edge cases**: `--client-crt` and `--client-crt-key` must be provided together (`--client-crt and --client-crt-key must always be specified together`); header values must be `Name:Value` (`additional headers must be colon(:)-separated: <x>`); headers are also applied to grpc-web and plain HTTP calls.
- **Configuration surface**: env `ARGOCD_OPTS`, `ARGOCD_SERVER`, `ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN`, `ARGOCD_SERVER_NAME`, `ARGOCD_APPLICATION_CONTROLLER_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REDIS_HAPROXY_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REDIS_NAME`, `ARGOCD_REPO_SERVER_NAME`, `REDIS_COMPRESSION`, `ARGOCD_GRPC_MAX_SIZE_MB`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `ARGOCD_OPTS="--server x --plaintext"` equivalent to flags; `--server=x` form in ARGOCD_OPTS works.
  - Negative: malformed ARGOCD_OPTS -> fatal message; header without colon -> error.
  - Positive: precedence flag > env > config verified for server and token.

### API-052 CLI local configuration file and contexts
- **Upstream**: [util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L16](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L16), [util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L270](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L270), [util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L326](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/localconfig/localconfig.go#L326), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L155](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L155)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Config file (YAML) `{current-context, contexts[{name, server, user}], servers[{server, plain-text, insecure, grpc-web, grpc-web-root-path, core, certificate-authority-data, client-certificate-data, client-certificate-key-data}], users[{name, auth-token, refresh-token}], prompts-enabled}`. Directory resolution: `ARGOCD_CONFIG_DIR` if set; else legacy `~/.argocd` if it exists; else `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/argocd`; else `~/.config/argocd`; file `config`. Server options from the resolved context (TLS CA, client certs (both or neither: error `ClientCertificateData and ClientCertificateKeyData must always be specified together`), plaintext, insecure, grpc-web) are used unless overridden by flags. If the stored token is expired and a refresh token exists, the CLI redeems it at the OIDC provider (using `SettingsService.Get` OIDC config) and rewrites the config file. `prompts-enabled` controls optional confirmation prompts (overridable by `--prompts-enabled`).
- **Edge cases**: unknown context name -> error from `ResolveContext`; config file absent is allowed when `--server` is given.
- **Configuration surface**: `--config`, `ARGOCD_CONFIG_DIR`, `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: login writes server/user/context and sets current-context; second login adds another context.
  - Positive: expired token + refresh token transparently refreshed and persisted.
  - Negative: half client cert data in config -> error.

### API-053 CLI transport selection: native gRPC, gRPC-Web fallback, TLS, headers, retries
- **Upstream**: [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L259](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L259), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L266](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L266), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L283](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L283), [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L907](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L907), [pkg/apiclient/grpcproxy.go#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/grpcproxy.go#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Without `--grpc-web`/`--grpc-web-root-path`, the server address gets `:443` appended if no port, and the CLI probes `VersionService.Version` over native gRPC; on failure it retries with gRPC-Web and, if that works, keeps using it and warns `Failed to invoke grpc call. Use flag --grpc-web in grpc calls...`. gRPC-Web mode tunnels gRPC through a local proxy over HTTP/1.1 (`grpcproxy.go`), honoring `--grpc-web-root-path`. TLS unless `--plaintext`; `--insecure` skips verification; `--server-crt` pins CA; `--client-crt/--client-crt-key` enable mTLS. `-H` headers and `User-Agent: argocd-client/<version>` are attached to every call (gRPC metadata and HTTP). `--http-retry-max N` wraps HTTP calls in a retryable client. Token is sent as gRPC metadata `token`.
- **Edge cases**: plaintext + grpc-web works for proxies terminating TLS; probe failures on both transports surface the original error later.
- **Configuration surface**: `--grpc-web`, `--grpc-web-root-path`, `--plaintext`, `--insecure`, `--server-crt`, `--client-crt`, `--client-crt-key`, `-H/--header`, `--http-retry-max`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: behind an HTTP/1-only proxy the CLI auto-falls back to grpc-web and succeeds.
  - Positive: `--grpc-web-root-path argo` reaches server under `/argo`.
  - Negative: TLS verification failure without `--insecure` fails.

### API-054 CLI port-forward mode
- **Upstream**: [pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L209](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go#L209), [cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L87](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L87)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `--port-forward` (or any `--port-forward-namespace`) opens a Kubernetes port-forward to port 8080 of a pod selected by `app.kubernetes.io/name=<--server-name>` in the given/current namespace (kubeconfig, `--kube-context`), sets server to `127.0.0.1:<random>` and forces `insecure`. `argocd login --port-forward` stores server name `port-forward`.
- **Edge cases**: no matching pod -> error; works together with `--plaintext` for `--insecure` servers.
- **Configuration surface**: `--port-forward`, `--port-forward-namespace`, `--server-name`/`ARGOCD_SERVER_NAME`, `--kube-context`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: commands succeed against an in-cluster server without ingress.
  - Negative: wrong `--server-name` -> clear pod-not-found error.

### API-055 CLI core (headless) mode
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/headless/headless.go#L180](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/headless/headless.go#L180), [cmd/argocd/commands/headless/headless.go#L318](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/headless/headless.go#L318), [cmd/argocd/commands/initialize/cmd.go#L22](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/initialize/cmd.go#L22), [docs/operator-manual/core.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/core.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: With `--core` (or a context whose server has `core: true`, e.g. after `argocd login --core`), commands start an in-process API server on `localhost:<random>` with auth disabled, using the kubeconfig credentials (namespace from kubeconfig/`-n`), an in-memory miniredis cache that forwards to the real Redis via port-forward (honoring `--redis-name`, `--redis-haproxy-name`, `--redis-compress`, Redis password from cluster secret), repo-server reached via port-forward (`--repo-server-name`), proxy extensions disabled, controller client in dry-run mode. The CLI then talks to it in plaintext; up to 5 attempts (1s apart) to reach `Version` before failing `all retries failed`. Kubernetes RBAC of the kubeconfig user is the effective authorization.
- **Edge cases**: unsupported kubectl flags in core mode: `disable-compression`, `certificate-authority`, `client-certificate`, `client-key`, `as`, `as-group`, `as-uid`, `tls-server-name`; `--context` (kube) flag change is honored; operations needing the controller (sync) still work via CR updates.
- **Configuration surface**: `--core`, `--kube-context`, kubectl flags, `--redis-*`, `--repo-server-name`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `argocd app list --core` lists apps with only kubeconfig access.
  - Negative: kubeconfig user lacking list permission -> error.
  - Recovery: redis not reachable -> commands not needing cache still work or fail with clear error.

### API-056 CLI plugins (`argocd-<name>` executables)
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L38](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L38), [cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L85](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L85), [cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L142](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/plugin.go#L142), [cmd/main.go#L87](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/main.go#L87), [cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L47](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/root.go#L47)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: When cobra reports `unknown command` for the `argocd` CLI, the first argument `<x>` is looked up as executable `argocd-<x>` on `PATH`; if found it is executed with the remaining args, current environment and stdio, and the CLI exits with the plugin's exit code (1 if not an exec error). Plugins found in relative paths (`exec.ErrDot`) are rejected with an error log. If no plugin exists the normal `Error: unknown command ... Run 'argocd --help' for usage.` is printed. Root-level tab completion lists available plugin names (executable files named `argocd-<name>` without path separators).
- **Edge cases**: built-in commands always win over plugins; flags before the plugin name are not supported (args[1:] used).
- **Configuration surface**: `PATH`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `argocd foo bar` runs `argocd-foo bar` and propagates exit code 3.
  - Negative: missing plugin -> unknown command message; plugin in `./` relative PATH entry rejected.

### API-057 Multi-binary dispatch (`ARGOCD_BINARY_NAME`)
- **Upstream**: [cmd/main.go#L28](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/main.go#L28), [cmd/main.go#L46](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/main.go#L46)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: One binary implements all components; it selects the command by `ARGOCD_BINARY_NAME` or `basename(argv[0])`: `argocd` (CLI), `argocd-server`, `argocd-application-controller`, `argocd-repo-server`, `argocd-cmp-server`, `argocd-commit-server`, `argocd-dex`, `argocd-notifications`, `argocd-git-ask-pass`, `argocd-applicationset-controller`, `argocd-k8s-auth`; any other name (e.g. `argocd-darwin-amd64`) runs the CLI. Plugin handling (API-056) applies only to CLI-like binaries.
- **Edge cases**: errors in CLI-like binaries are printed by the plugin handler (cobra error output silenced).
- **Configuration surface**: `ARGOCD_BINARY_NAME`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: symlink named `argocd-server` starts the API server; `argocd-linux-amd64` behaves as CLI.

### API-058 `argocd login`, `argocd logout`, `argocd relogin`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/login.go#L51](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/login.go#L51), [cmd/argocd/commands/logout.go#L27](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/logout.go#L27), [cmd/argocd/commands/logout.go#L113](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/logout.go#L113), [cmd/argocd/commands/relogin.go#L30](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/relogin.go#L30), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_login.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_login.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `login SERVER` (SERVER optional with `--port-forward` -> `port-forward`, `--core` -> `kubernetes`): unless `--skip-test-tls`, probes TLS (30s); if server has no TLS and `--plaintext` not set, prompts `WARNING: server is not configured with TLS. Proceed (y/n)?`; cert error prompts to proceed insecurely. Password login prompts for missing username/password and calls `SessionService.Create`; `--sso` runs an OAuth2 authorization-code (PKCE when enabled) flow with a local callback server on `--sso-port` (default 8085) or `--callback` URL, launching the browser unless `--sso-launch-browser=false`, storing id_token and refresh_token. Prints `'<email|name|sub>' logged in successfully` and `Context '<name>' updated`; context name = `--name` or server (plus `/<grpc-web-root-path>`). `--core` login stores a core context without contacting a server. `logout CONTEXT` asks confirmation (prompts), POSTs to `<scheme>://<context>/auth/logout` with the token cookie to revoke it server-side (warnings on failure), then removes the token from local config. `relogin` re-authenticates the current context (password or SSO according to stored token issuer).
- **Edge cases**: logout of unknown context -> fatal `Context <x> does not exist`; no local config -> `Nothing to logout from`.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd login SERVER` | Log in to Argo CD | `--callback`, `--name`, `--password`, `--skip-test-tls`, `--sso`, `--sso-launch-browser (default true)`, `--sso-port (default 8085)`, `--username` | [argocd_login.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_login.md#L1) |
| `argocd logout CONTEXT` | Log out from Argo CD | — | [argocd_logout.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_logout.md#L1) |
| `argocd relogin` | Refresh an expired authenticate token | `--callback`, `--password`, `--sso-launch-browser (default true)`, `--sso-port (default 8085)` | [argocd_relogin.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_relogin.md#L1) |

- **Configuration surface**: local config (API-052), `--sso-port`, `--callback`.
- **Exposure**: CLI (+ API SessionService, `/auth/logout`, OIDC).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: password login creates context; SSO login with mocked IdP stores refresh token; relogin refreshes token.
  - Negative: wrong password -> non-zero exit; non-TLS server without `--plaintext` and declined prompt -> exit 1.
  - Positive: logout revokes token (subsequent API call with old token fails) and removes it locally.

### API-059 `argocd context`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/context.go#L23](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/context.go#L23), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_context.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_context.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Alias `ctx`. No args: lists contexts (current marked). `context NAME` switches current context (unknown -> fatal), writing the previous one to `<configDir>/.prev-ctx`; `context -` switches back to the previous. `--delete NAME` removes context, its user and server; deletes the file if empty; clears current-context if it was current. Already-current prints `Already at context '<x>'`.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd context [CONTEXT]` | Switch between contexts | `--delete` | [argocd_context.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_context.md#L1) |

- **Edge cases**: no local config -> `No local configuration found` exit 1.
- **Configuration surface**: local config.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: switch, switch back with `-`, delete current, delete last (file removed).

### API-060 `argocd configure`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/configure.go#L20](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/configure.go#L20), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_configure.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_configure.md#L1)

- **Semantics**: Persists `prompts-enabled` into the local config and prints `prompts-enabled: <bool>`.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd configure` | Manage local configuration | `--prompts-enabled` + kubectl client flags | [argocd_configure.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_configure.md#L1) |

- **Edge cases**: requires an existing local config.
- **Configuration surface**: `prompts-enabled`.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: toggling affects confirmation prompts of `logout`, `app delete`, etc.

### API-061 `argocd version`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/version.go#L20](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/version.go#L20), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_version.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_version.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Prints client version and (unless `--client`) server version via `VersionService.Version`; `--short` prints only version strings; `-o json|yaml|wide|short`.

|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd version` | Print version information | `--client`, `-o/--output (default wide)`, `--short` + kubectl client flags | [argocd_version.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_version.md#L1) |

### API-062 `argocd completion`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/completion.go#L198](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/completion.go#L198), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_completion.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_completion.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Emits completion script for `bash`, `zsh`, `fish` or `powershell`; other values print `Invalid shell '<x>'. The supported shells are bash, zsh and fish.` and exit 1. Dynamic completion includes app names, contexts and plugin names.
