- **Edge cases**: missing argument prints help and exits 1.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: each shell produces non-empty script; invalid shell exit 1.

### API-063 `argocd app` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L61](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L61), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L126](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L126), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L343](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L343), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L532](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L532), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1418](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1418), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1597](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1597), [cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1735](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app.go#L1735), [cmd/argocd/commands/app_diff.go#L646](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app_diff.go#L646), [cmd/argocd/commands/app_resources.go#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app_resources.go#L45), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_app.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_app.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Wraps ApplicationService. Subcommands: `create` (flags or `-f` file(s)/URL; `--upsert`; `--set-finalizer`) -> `Create`; `get` (`--refresh`/`--hard-refresh`, `--show-params`, `--show-operation`, `--timeout`, `-o json|yaml|wide|tree|tree=detailed`, multi-source `--source-position/--source-name`) -> `Get` + `ResourceTree`; `list` (`-p/--project`, `-l/--selector`, `-r/--repo`, `-P/--path`, `-c/--cluster`, `-N/--app-namespace`, `-o wide|name|json|yaml`) -> `List`; `set`/`unset`/`add-source`/`remove-source` modify spec via `UpdateSpec`; `edit` opens `$EDITOR` on the spec then `UpdateSpec`; `patch` (`--patch`, `--type json|merge`, default json) -> `Patch`; `delete` (`--cascade` default true, `-p/--propagation-policy` foreground|background, `-l` selector, `--wait`, `-y`) -> `Delete` with confirmation prompt; `sync` (selectors `-l`, `--project`, revisions, `--resource GROUP:KIND:[NS/]NAME` incl. `!` exclusion and `*`, `--label`, `--local`, `--prune`, `--dry-run`, `--force`, `--replace`, `--server-side`, `--apply-out-of-sync-only`, `--strategy`, retry flags, `--info`, `--preview-changes`, `--async`, `--timeout`) -> `Sync` then watches until operation completes; `wait` (`--sync`, `--health`, `--suspended`, `--degraded`, `--delete`, `--hydrated`, `--operation`, `--resource`, `-l/--selector`, `--timeout`) watches until conditions hold; `history`, `rollback APPNAME [ID]` (default previous), `terminate-op`, `manifests` (`--source git|live`, default git; `--revision(s)`, `--local` + `--local-repo-root` render locally), `diff` (exit codes: 2 on general errors, `--diff-exit-code` (default 1) when a diff is found, 0 when none; `--exit-code=false` disables; `--local`/`--local-include`/`--local-repo-root` stream local files through `GetManifestsWithFiles` or generate locally; `--revision(s)`, `--refresh`, `--hard-refresh`, `--server-side-generate`, `--server-side-diff` (+concurrency/batch flags); uses `KUBECTL_EXTERNAL_DIFF`; Secrets ignored), `logs` (`--group/--kind/--name/--namespace`, `-c/--container`, `-f/--follow`, `--tail`, `--since-seconds`, `--until-time`, `--filter`, `-m/--match-case`, `-p/--previous`), `resources` (`--orphaned`, `--output tree|tree=detailed`, `--project`), `get-resource` (`--filter-fields`, `--show-managed-fields`, `-o`), `patch-resource`, `delete-resource` (`--force`, `--orphan`, `--all`), `confirm-deletion` (sets deletion-approved annotation for resources requiring confirmation), `actions list|run` (API-064).

- **Edge cases**: `app sync --local DIR` generates manifests client-side (local Kustomize/Helm tooling) and sends them as `manifests` (server requires `override`; CLI refuses with `Cannot use local sync when Automatic Sync Policy is enabled except with --dry-run`, and `argocd cli does not work on multi-source app with --local flag`); `--app-namespace/-N` or `ns/name` form address apps in other namespaces; `app delete` prompts for confirmation unless `-y`.
- **Configuration surface**: `KUBECTL_EXTERNAL_DIFF`, `EDITOR`/`KUBE_EDITOR`.
- **Exposure**: CLI (ApplicationService).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: golden output for `app get`, `app list -o name`, `app history`; full create->sync->wait->rollback->delete flow.
  - Negative: `app diff` exit codes 0/1/2; `app sync` on unknown app exits non-zero with PermissionDenied text.
  - Authorization: commands surface server PermissionDenied unchanged.

### API-064 `argocd app actions` subgroup
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L48](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L48), [cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L70](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L70), [cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L154](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/app_actions.go#L154), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_app_actions.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_app_actions.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `actions list APPNAME` filters managed resources by `--group/--kind/--resource-name/--namespace` and lists available actions (`-o` table/json/yaml). `actions run APPNAME ACTION` runs the action on all matching resources (`--all` required to run on multiple), using `RunResourceActionV2` (falls back to V1 for older servers).
- **Edge cases**: multiple matches without `--all` -> error; no matching resources -> error.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: list for Deployment shows `restart`; run restart; ambiguous match without `--all` fails.

### API-065 `argocd appset` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L52](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L52), [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L76](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L76), [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L140](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L140), [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L252](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L252), [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L334](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L334), [cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L379](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/applicationset.go#L379), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `create FILE...` (YAML/JSON, multiple documents; `--upsert`, `--dry-run` prints generated applications, `--wait` waits for status) -> `ApplicationSetService.Create`; `get NAME` (`--show-params` shows generator params) ; `list` (`-p`, `-l`, `-N`); `delete NAME...` (confirmation unless `-y`; `--wait`); `generate FILE` -> `Generate` printing generated Applications.

| `argocd appset create` | Create one or more ApplicationSets | `-N/--appset-namespace`, `--dry-run`, `-o/--output (default wide)`, `--upsert`, `--wait` | [argocd_appset_create.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset_create.md#L1) |
| `argocd appset delete` | Delete one or more ApplicationSets | `-N/--appset-namespace`, `--wait`, `-y/--yes` | [argocd_appset_delete.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset_delete.md#L1) |
| `argocd appset generate` | Generate apps of ApplicationSet rendered templates | `-N/--appset-namespace`, `-o/--output (default wide)` | [argocd_appset_generate.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset_generate.md#L1) |
| `argocd appset get APPSETNAME` | Get ApplicationSet details | `-N/--appset-namespace`, `-o/--output (default wide)`, `--show-params` | [argocd_appset_get.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset_get.md#L1) |
| `argocd appset list` | List ApplicationSets | `-N/--appset-namespace`, `-o/--output (default wide)`, `-p/--project`, `-l/--selector` | [argocd_appset_list.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_appset_list.md#L1) |

- **Edge cases**: templated project rejected by server; `-N` for appsets in other namespaces.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: create from multi-doc file, dry-run shows apps without creating, generate output, delete confirmation.

### API-066 `argocd proj` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L44](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L44), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L113](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L113), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L148](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L148), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L189](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L189), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L659](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L659), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L1120](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L1120), [cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L1189](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project.go#L1189), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_proj.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_proj.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA (`add-signature-key`/`remove-signature-key` Deprecated)
- **Semantics**: Alias `project`. `create` (flags or `-f`, `--upsert`), `set`, `get`, `list`, `edit` ($EDITOR), `delete`, `add/remove-destination` (server or name + namespace, `--name` to treat as cluster name), `add/remove-destination-service-account`, `add/remove-source`, `add/remove-source-namespace`, `add/remove-orphaned-ignore`, `allow-cluster-resource PROJECT GROUP KIND [NAME]` / `deny-cluster-resource`, `allow|deny-namespace-resource GROUP KIND` (`-l/--list allow|deny` selects which list is edited; default `allow` for cluster-scoped, `deny` for namespaced), `add/remove-signature-key` (deprecated: `Managing project signature keys through CLI is deprecated, migrate to Source Integrity defined in AppProject manifest`). All mutating subcommands do Get -> modify -> `ProjectService.Update`.

- **Edge cases**: `add-destination` of an existing destination fails (`Specified destination is already defined in project`) and `remove-destination` of a missing one fails (`Specified destination does not exist in project`); `add-source` of an already allowed repo only prints `Source repository '<x>' already allowed in project`.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: each mutator round-trips through `get -o yaml`; deprecated commands print deprecation warning.

### API-069 `argocd proj source-integrity git policies` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L57](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L57), [cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L105](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L105), [cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L264](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L264), [cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L361](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/project_source_integrity.go#L361), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_proj_source-integrity.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_proj_source-integrity.md#L1)

### API-070 `argocd repo` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L28](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L28), [cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L114](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L114), [cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L289](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L289), [cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L362](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L362), [cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L445](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/repo.go#L445), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_repo.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_repo.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `add REPOURL` builds a Repository (type git|helm|oci, auth via username/password, SSH key file, TLS client cert, GitHub App, GCP SA key, Azure WI/SP, bearer token, proxy/no-proxy, depth, LFS, project scope) and calls `ValidateAccess` then `CreateRepository` (`--upsert`); `get REPO` (`--refresh`, `--project`, `-o`), `list` (`--refresh`, `-o yaml|json|url|wide`), `rm REPO...` (`--project`). Write repositories are not manageable from the CLI.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd repo` | Manage repository connection parameters | — + kubectl client flags | [argocd_repo.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_repo.md#L1) |

### API-072 `argocd cluster` command group
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L52](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L52), [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L93](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L93), [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L243](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L243), [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L327](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L327), [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L402](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L402), [cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L551](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/cluster.go#L551), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `add CONTEXT` reads the kubeconfig context, (unless exec/AWS/in-cluster auth) installs `argocd-manager` ServiceAccount + ClusterRole(Binding) (or namespace-scoped Role when `--namespace` given) in `--system-namespace`, obtains a bearer token and calls `ClusterService.Create` (`--upsert`; confirmation unless `-y`); `get SERVER/NAME` (`-o json|yaml|wide|server`, default yaml), `list`, `set NAME` (`--name`, `--namespace`, `--label`, `--annotation`) -> `Update` with `updatedFields`, `rm SERVER/NAME...` (prompt `Are you sure you want to remove '<x>'? Any Apps deploying to this cluster will go to health status Unknown.[y/n]`, with `A` = yes to all when several; `-y` skips), `rotate-auth SERVER/NAME`.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd cluster` | Manage cluster credentials | — + kubectl client flags | [argocd_cluster.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster add CONTEXT` | argocd cluster add CONTEXT | `--annotation`, `--aws-cluster-name`, `--aws-profile`, `--aws-role-arn`, `--cluster-endpoint`, `--cluster-resources`, `--disable-compression`, `--exec-command`, `--exec-command-api-version`, `--exec-command-args`, `--exec-command-env`, `--exec-command-install-hint`, `--in-cluster`, `--kubeconfig`, `--label`, `--name`, `--namespace`, `--project`, `--proxy-url`, `--service-account`, `--shard (default -1)`, `--system-namespace (default kube-system)`, `--upsert`, `-y/--yes` | [argocd_cluster_add.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_add.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster get SERVER/NAME` | Get cluster information | `-o/--output (default yaml)` | [argocd_cluster_get.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_get.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster list` | List configured clusters | `-o/--output (default wide)` | [argocd_cluster_list.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_list.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster rm SERVER/NAME` | Remove cluster credentials | `-y/--yes` | [argocd_cluster_rm.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_rm.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster rotate-auth SERVER/NAME` | argocd cluster rotate-auth SERVER/NAME | — | [argocd_cluster_rotate-auth.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_rotate-auth.md#L1) |
| `argocd cluster set NAME` | Set cluster information | `--annotation`, `--label`, `--name`, `--namespace` | [argocd_cluster_set.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_cluster_set.md#L1) |

- **Edge cases**: `--cluster-endpoint kubeconfig|kube-public|internal`; `--in-cluster` uses `https://kubernetes.default.svc`; `--shard` default -1 (unset).
- **Configuration surface**: kubeconfig.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: add against kind cluster creates SA; set renames; rotate-auth changes token; rm.

### API-073 `argocd account` command group (incl. `bcrypt`)
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L38](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L38), [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L77](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L77), [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L161](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L161), [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L212](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L212), [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L383](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L383), [cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L458](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/account.go#L458), [cmd/argocd/commands/bcrypt.go#L17](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/bcrypt.go#L17), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_account.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_account.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `update-password` (prompts for current/new password, `--account` for another user; after self-change re-logs in and updates the stored token), `get-user-info` (alias `whoami`; `-o`), `can-i ACTION RESOURCE SUBRESOURCE` (prints yes/no), `list`, `get` (`-a`), `generate-token` (`-a`, `-e` duration, `--id`; prints token), `delete-token ID` (`-a`), `session-token` (prints current context token, `-o`), `bcrypt --password` (local bcrypt hash generation, no server call).

| `argocd account bcrypt` | Generate bcrypt hash for any password | `--password` | [argocd_account_bcrypt.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_account_bcrypt.md#L1) |

| `argocd account generate-token` | Generate account token | `-a/--account`, `-e/--expires-in (default 0s)`, `--id` | [argocd_account_generate-token.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_account_generate-token.md#L1) |

- **Edge cases**: `can-i` prints `yes`/`no` (exit 0 in both cases); `bcrypt` without `--password` prompts or reads stdin; `update-password` without flags prompts for current and new (confirmed) password and, when changing one's own password, logs in again and stores the new token in the local config.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: whoami alias; can-i yes/no; token lifecycle; bcrypt hash verifies with bcrypt compare.

### API-077 `argocd admin app`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L53](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L53), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L89](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L89), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L172](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L172), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L262](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/app.go#L262), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_app.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_app.md#L1)

### API-078 `argocd admin cluster` (incl. `namespaces`)
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L45), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L188](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L188), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L312](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L312), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L467](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L467), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L523](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L523), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L576](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/cluster.go#L576), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_cluster.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_cluster.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `generate-spec CONTEXT` (cluster Secret YAML; optional `--generate-bearer-token`), `kubeconfig CLUSTER_URL OUTPUT_PATH` (writes kubeconfig from stored cluster secret), `namespaces` (prints managed namespaces per cluster), `namespaces enable-namespaced-mode PATTERN` / `disable-namespaced-mode PATTERN` (`--dry-run` default true; `--max-namespace-count`, `--cluster-resources`), `shards` and `stats` (read controller cache in Redis — port-forwarded by default — to print shard distribution/statistics with `--sharding-method`, `--replicas`, `--shard`).

### API-079 `argocd admin dashboard`

### API-080 `argocd admin export` / `argocd admin import`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/backup.go#L41](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/backup.go#L41), [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/backup.go#L173](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/backup.go#L173), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_export.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_export.md#L1), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_import.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_import.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `export` writes a multi-document YAML of `argocd-cm`, `argocd-rbac-cm`, `argocd-ssh-known-hosts-cm`, `argocd-tls-certs-cm`, Argo CD Secrets (`argocd-secret` and secrets labeled as repository/cluster/repo-creds), AppProjects, Applications and ApplicationSets (also from `--application-namespaces`/`--applicationset-namespaces` globs) to stdout or `-o FILE`. `import SOURCE` (`-` for stdin) applies them: creates/updates, `--prune` deletes live objects absent from the backup, `--dry-run`, `--override-on-conflict`, `--skip-resources-with-label`, `--stop-operation` (clears in-flight operations), `--ignore-tracking`, `--verbose`, `--prompts-enabled`.
- **Edge cases**: import preserves/updates only Argo CD-owned secrets (`isArgoCDSecret`); status fields handled per kind.

| Command (usage) | Purpose | Own flags (defaults) | Doc |
|---|---|---|---|
| `argocd admin export` | Export all Argo CD data to stdout (default) or a file | `--application-namespaces`, `--applicationset-namespaces`, `-o/--out (default -)` + kubectl client flags | [argocd_admin_export.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_export.md#L1) |
| `argocd admin import SOURCE` | Import Argo CD data from stdin (specify `-') or a file | `--application-namespaces`, `--applicationset-namespaces`, `--dry-run`, `--ignore-tracking`, `--override-on-conflict`, `--prompts-enabled`, `--prune`, `--skip-resources-with-label`, `--stop-operation`, `--verbose` + kubectl client flags | [argocd_admin_import.md](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_import.md#L1) |

- **Configuration surface**: kubeconfig.
- **Exposure**: CLI.
- **Tests required**: export->import round-trip into empty namespace reproduces objects; `--prune` removes extras; `--dry-run` no changes.

### API-082 `argocd admin notifications`
- **Upstream**: [cmd/argocd/commands/admin/admin.go#L81](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/cmd/argocd/commands/admin/admin.go#L81), [docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_notifications.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/commands/argocd_admin_notifications.md#L1)

- **Tests required**: trigger run prints condition result; template notify to console renders message.

## Part C — Web UI (`ui/src/app`)

UI routes (served by API-013 SPA fallback, relative to base href): `/login`, `/applications`, `/applications/:name`, `/applications/:appnamespace/:name`, `/applications/[:appnamespace/]:name/:namespace/:container/logs`, `/applicationsets`, `/applicationsets/[:appnamespace/]:name`, `/settings`, `/settings/{repos,certs,gpgkeys,clusters,clusters/:server,projects,projects/:name,accounts,accounts/:name,appearance}`, `/user-info`, `/help`, plus routes added by system-level extensions; `/` redirects to `/applications`.

### API-086 UI shell: routing, navigation sidebar, session bootstrap, version panel
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L39](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L39), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L56](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L56), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L181](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L181), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L212](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L212), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L342](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L342), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-container.tsx#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-container.tsx#L13), [ui/src/app/settings/components/settings-container.tsx#L17](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/settings-container.tsx#L17), [ui/src/app/shared/components/page/page.tsx#L30](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/page/page.tsx#L30), [ui/src/app/shared/components/version-info/version-info-panel.tsx#L70](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/version-info/version-info-panel.tsx#L70)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: On load the UI fetches settings (`GET /api/v1/settings`), user info (`GET /api/v1/session/userinfo`; 401 treated as logged-out) and version in parallel. Logged-out users are redirected: if SSO is configured (Dex connectors or OIDC) to `<base>/auth/login?return_url=<current URL>`, else to `/login?return_url=...`. If version info includes Kustomize/Helm versions while not logged in, the UI assumes auth is disabled and proceeds. Any later API 401 triggers the same redirect (SSO redirect de-duplicated for 5s). Sidebar nav items: Applications, ApplicationSets, Settings, User Info, Documentation (+ system extensions); sidebar can be collapsed (`hideSidebar` preference). Page header shows `Log out` (navigates to `/auth/logout`) or `Log in`. Clicking the version opens a panel listing all version fields with a copy-to-clipboard button. Uncaught render errors show "Something went wrong!" with stack and an issue link.
- **Edge cases**: favicon/assets resolved relative to base href; `return_url` preserved through login.
- **Configuration surface**: `--basehref`; SSO settings.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: unauthenticated visit to `/applications` lands on `/login?return_url=...`; with SSO lands on `/auth/login?...`.
  - Positive: all nav items route correctly; version panel copy.
  - Recovery: session expiry (401 on SSE) redirects to login once.

### API-087 UI login page
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L21](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L21), [ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L71](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L71), [ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L94](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/login/components/login.tsx#L94)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Shows SSO button when Dex has connectors or OIDC is configured (`Log in via <oidc name>`, or `Log in via <connector>` when exactly one Dex connector, else `SSO Login`) linking to `auth/login?return_url=`; shows username/password form (both required) unless `userLoginsDisabled`; submits `POST /api/v1/session` and navigates to `return_url`. When local logins disabled and no SSO, shows a message. SSO errors arrive via `has_sso_error=true`/`sso_error` query params and are displayed.
- **Edge cases**: invalid credentials display server error message.
- **Configuration surface**: `dex.config`, `oidc.config`, accounts with `login` capability.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: form validation messages; successful login redirect; SSO button label variants; logins-disabled message.

### API-088 UI applications list — views, pagination, sorting, health status bar
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L29](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L29), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L34](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L34), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L520](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L520), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/view-type-switcher.tsx#L14](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/view-type-switcher.tsx#L14), [ui/src/app/shared/components/paginate/paginate.tsx#L31](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/paginate/paginate.tsx#L31), [ui/src/app/shared/components/paginate/paginate.tsx#L84](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/paginate/paginate.tsx#L84)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Loads apps with `GET /api/v1/applications?fields=<projection>` (filtered server-side by selected projects and `appNamespace` query) and then live-updates via `GET /api/v1/stream/applications?resourceVersion=...&fields=...` with events buffered for 500 ms. Views (`view` URL param + persisted preference): `tiles` (default), `list` (table), `summary` (pie charts/totals by sync/health). Pagination with page size 5/10/15/20/all (default 10; `page` URL param) and sort options `Name` (natural numeric compare), `Created At` (desc), `Synchronized` (operation finishedAt desc); a toggleable health status bar summarises filtered apps. Each tile/row shows project, labels, status, repo/path/target revision, destination, notice icon (API-117), links dropdown, and per-app actions Sync / Refresh / Delete. App-of-apps detection marks parent apps.
- **Edge cases**: page visibility handling pauses refresh while hidden; empty state "Create new application to start managing resources in your cluster" vs "No matching applications found" with "clear filters".
- **Configuration surface**: browser preferences (API-119).
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: switch views (URL updates), change page size/sort (persisted), status bar toggle.
  - Positive: list updates live on app create/delete without reload.
  - Authorization: only apps the user can get are shown.

### API-089 UI applications list — search and filters (URL-synchronized), favorites
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L102](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L102), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L197](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L197), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L236](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L236), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L71](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L71), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L522](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L522)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Search box (`search` URL param; `/` focuses, `Esc` blurs) matches substring of app name or namespace. Filters (each reflected in URL params and persisted): Favorites only (`showFavorites`), SYNC STATUS (`sync`), HEALTH STATUS (`health`), OPERATION STATUS (`operation`; Deleting/Terminated counted as Syncing), LABELS (`labels`; `key`, `key=value`, `!key` etc. selector expressions), ANNOTATIONS (`annotations`), PROJECTS (`proj`; drives server query), CLUSTERS (`cluster`; `name (url)`, URL, or glob on name), NAMESPACES (`namespace`; glob on destination namespace), REPOSITORIES (`repo`; glob on any source repoURL), TARGET REVISION (`targetRevision`; glob), AUTO SYNC (`autoSync` Enabled/Disabled). Filter options show counts computed against the other active filters; "clear filters" resets all. Favorites are toggled per app (star) and stored in preferences (`favoritesAppList`).
- **Edge cases**: comma-separated URL values are URI-decoded for labels/annotations/repo/targetRevision; automated sync with `enabled: false` counts as Disabled.
- **Configuration surface**: preferences.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: each filter narrows results and serializes to URL; reload restores from URL.
  - Positive: glob filters (`prod-*`) on cluster/namespace/repo/revision.
  - Negative: filter with no matches shows empty-state with clear filters.

### API-090 UI applications list — New App, Sync Apps and Refresh Apps panels
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L452](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx#L452), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-sync-panel/applications-sync-panel.tsx#L79](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-sync-panel/applications-sync-panel.tsx#L79), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-refresh-panel/applications-refresh-panel.tsx#L41](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-refresh-panel/applications-refresh-panel.tsx#L41)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Toolbar actions: `New App` (opens creation panel, URL `?new={}`), `Sync Apps` (`?syncApps=true`: select apps from the filtered list, flags Prune / Dry Run / Apply Only / Force, sync options, retry; issues `Sync` per app, Force asks for confirmation with the force warning), `Refresh Apps` (`?refreshApps=true`: select apps, refresh type `normal`|`hard`; issues `GET /applications/{name}?refresh=<type>` per app). Errors per app are reported as notifications.
- **Edge cases**: closing create panel with unsaved input asks `Closing this panel will discard all entered values. Continue?`.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: bulk sync of 3 apps with prune; bulk hard refresh; partial failures surfaced.

### API-091 UI application creation wizard
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L326](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L326), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L330](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L330), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L354](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L354), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L377](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L377), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L437](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L437), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L461](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-create-panel/application-create-panel.tsx#L461)
- **Maturity**: GA (hydrator section Beta)
- **Semantics**: Sliding panel form with sections GENERAL (Application Name, Project Name (from project list), Sync Policy Manual/Automatic with Prune Resources / Self Heal, set deletion finalizer checkbox, sync options (API-098 options), retry), SOURCE / SOURCES (multi-source collapsible sections; repository URL with type git/helm/oci suggestions from configured repos, revision with branches/tags from `ListRefs`, path suggestions from `ListApps` or chart/version from `GetHelmCharts`), optional Hydrator source (dry source / sync source / hydrate-to branch) when hydrator enabled, DESTINATION (Cluster URL or Cluster Name, Namespace), and tool-specific parameters (Directory / Helm / Kustomize / Plugin) loaded via `GetAppDetails`. `Edit as YAML` toggles a YAML editor bound to the same object. `Create` posts `POST /api/v1/applications`.
- **Edge cases**: validation errors from server shown inline/notification; switching YAML/form preserves values.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: create git directory app; create Helm chart app with values; multi-source app; YAML mode round-trip; server validation error displayed.

### API-092 UI application details — views, filtering, grouping, zoom, defaults
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L627](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L627), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L638](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L638), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L651](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L651), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L742](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L742), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1443](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1443), [ui/src/app/shared/models.ts#L166](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/models.ts#L166), [docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L7](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L7)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Views selectable via icons and `view` URL param: Tree (default), Pods (group by `node`, `parentResource`, `topLevelResource`; `podSortMode` param), Network (networking hierarchy), List (paged resource table), plus App View extensions (API-123). Default view per app via annotation `pref.argocd.argoproj.io/default-view` (`tree|pods|network|list`) and pod grouping via `pref.argocd.argoproj.io/default-pod-sort`, both overridden by URL/preferences. Resource filter sidebar: name (wildcards), kind, sync status (OutOfSync includes Unknown), health (node or root), namespace; `resource` URL param. Toggles: show orphaned resources (`orphaned` param), Group Nodes / Collapse Pods, zoom in/out (persisted), node label propagation in pod view (`application.allowedNodeLabels`). The tree is live via `WatchResourceTree` SSE.
- **Edge cases**: hooks always shown regardless of sync filter; apps without source disable Details/Diff/Sync actions.
- **Configuration surface**: annotations `pref.argocd.argoproj.io/default-view`, `pref.argocd.argoproj.io/default-pod-sort`; argocd-cm `application.allowedNodeLabels`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: each view renders; annotation default view honored; orphaned toggle shows orphaned nodes; filters via URL.

### API-093 UI application details — action bar and app-level operations
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1318](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1318), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1344](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1344), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L276](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L276)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Buttons: Details (opens app summary panel), Diff (disabled when Synced), Sync (opens sync panel), Toggle Auto-Sync (confirmation, runs resource action `toggle-auto-sync` on the Application), Confirm Pruning (when a running op has resources requiring deletion confirmation), Sync Status (operation state panel), History and rollback, Delete (dialog choosing propagation Foreground / Background / Non-cascading; replaced by Confirm Deletion when deletion awaits confirmation), Refresh (normal; spinner while `argocd.argoproj.io/refresh` annotation present) with Hard Refresh sub-action. Top-bar extensions appear here (API-125). Rollback confirmation: if automated sync is enabled the UI first disables it (`spec.syncPolicy.automated.enabled=false` via Update with validate=false) after warning, then calls `Rollback`.
- **Edge cases**: Delete disabled while deletionTimestamp set; refresh button disabled while refreshing.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: each action triggers expected API call; rollback with autosync disables it first; confirm deletion sets approval annotation.

### API-094 UI application summary panel (app-level tabs)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L180](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L180), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L96](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L96), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L123](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L123), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L269](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L269), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L754](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L754), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L815](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/application-summary.tsx#L815)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Tabs: SUMMARY (editable: project, labels, annotations, notification subscriptions (`notifications.argoproj.io/subscribe.<trigger>.<service>` annotations with trigger/service pickers from NotificationService), cluster, namespace, revision history limit, sync options, retry options; read-only status/health/images; LINKS from `ListLinks`; sync policy toggles ENABLE AUTO-SYNC / PRUNE RESOURCES / SELF HEAL; source fields; hydrator section; Status Badge panel), PARAMETERS (single source) or SOURCES (multi-source, paged), MANIFEST (YAML editor of `spec`, saved via `UpdateSpec` with merge-patch applied client-side), DIFF (only when not Synced; managed resources diff), EVENTS (app events), plus resource-tab extensions registered for `argoproj.io/Application`.
- **Edge cases**: edits validated server-side (`validate` query) and errors shown; switching edit mode resets notification-subscription draft.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: edit labels/annotations and save; toggle auto-sync/prune/self-heal; manifest edit; notification subscription add/remove.

[ui/src/app/applications/components/application-node-info/application-node-info.tsx#L304](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-node-info/application-node-info.tsx#L304)

- **Configuration surface**: `exec.enabled`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: live manifest edit patches resource; logs tab hidden without logs permission; terminal tab hidden when exec disabled; action menu runs `restart`.

### API-096 UI pod logs viewer (incl. fullscreen route)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L272](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L272), [ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L286](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L286), [ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L299](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-logs-viewer/pod-logs-viewer.tsx#L299), [ui/src/app/shared/services/applications-service.ts#L326](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/applications-service.ts#L326), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-container.tsx#L25](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-container.tsx#L25)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Streams logs over SSE from `/api/v1/applications/{name}/logs` with controls: follow (default on) + auto-scroll, show previous logs, highlight pod, container selector (containers and init containers), since-seconds selector, tail lines, text filter (with `!` inversion) and match-case, wrap lines, show pod names, show timestamps, dark mode, copy, download (`download=true` link), fullscreen (route `/applications/[ns/]name/:namespace/:container/logs`). Many controls mirror to URL query params.
- **Edge cases**: aggregated logs for Deployments across pods; server limit on pod count surfaces as error.
- **Configuration surface**: `server.maxPodLogsToRender`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: follow streaming; filter; download file name; fullscreen route deep link.

### API-097 UI web terminal
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-terminal-viewer/pod-terminal-viewer.tsx#L68](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-terminal-viewer/pod-terminal-viewer.tsx#L68), [ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-terminal-viewer/pod-terminal-viewer.tsx#L171](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/pod-terminal-viewer/pod-terminal-viewer.tsx#L171), [docs/operator-manual/web_based_terminal.md#L16](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/web_based_terminal.md#L16)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: xterm-based terminal opening `ws(s)://<host><base>/terminal?pod=&container=&appName=&appNamespace=&projectName=&namespace=`; sends `{operation:"stdin", data, rows, cols}` and `{operation:"resize", rows, cols}`; renders `stdout` messages; handles reconnect code by reopening; container switcher for multi-container pods.
- **Edge cases**: only visible when exec enabled and permitted; closes on permission loss.
- **Configuration surface**: `exec.enabled`, `exec.shells`.
- **Exposure**: UI (+API-044).
- **Tests required**: interactive command echo; resize; reconnect after token refresh.

### API-098 UI sync panel (manual sync)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L104](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L104), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L119](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L119), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L147](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-options/application-sync-options.tsx#L147), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-panel/application-sync-panel.tsx#L166](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-sync-panel/application-sync-panel.tsx#L166)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Sync panel: revision (per source for multi-source), manual flags `Prune`, `Dry Run`, `Apply Only` (apply strategy without hooks), `Force`; sync options `Skip Schema Validation` (Validate=false), `Auto-Create Namespace`, `Apply Out of Sync Only`, `Respect Ignore Differences`, `Server-Side Apply`, `Prune Last`, `Prune Propagation Policy` (foreground|background|orphan), `Prune` (true|false|confirm), and `Replace` only when settings `syncWithReplaceAllowed` is true; retry options (limit, backoff duration/factor/max, refresh); resource checklist for partial sync (with "all/out of sync/none" selection). Warnings: prune-all, prune-some, replace and force (destructive) require confirmation. Issues `POST .../sync`.
- **Edge cases**: deep link `?deploy=<resourceKey>` preselects a single resource; sync blocked by window shows server error.
- **Configuration surface**: `--sync-with-replace-allowed`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: partial sync of one resource; Replace hidden when disallowed; force confirmation.

### API-099 UI history and rollback panel
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-deployment-history/application-deployment-history.tsx#L67](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-deployment-history/application-deployment-history.tsx#L67), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1228](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1228)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Lists `status.history` entries (deployed at, revision(s) with revision metadata via API-025, initiated by, duration, source parameters via `GetAppDetails` with `versionId`); each entry offers `Rollback` (older entries) or `Redeploy` (latest); opens via action bar (URL `rollback=<index>`).
- **Edge cases**: multi-source history shows each source; disabled when no operation state.
- **Configuration surface**: `spec.revisionHistoryLimit`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: rollback to previous revision; redeploy latest; metadata shown.

### API-100 UI operation state, conditions and status panel
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L989](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L989), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1238](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1238), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1249](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1249), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-status-panel/application-status-panel.tsx#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-status-panel/application-status-panel.tsx#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Status panel at top of details: App Health, Sync Status (with revision metadata, auto-sync indicator, sync window state), Last Sync (operation result, initiator), hydrator status when applicable, conditions count, status-panel extensions. Operation state panel (`operation=true` param) lists phase, message, start/finish, per-resource results; hydration operation panel (`hydrateOperation=true`); conditions panel (`conditions=true`) lists app conditions by type/message.
- **Edge cases**: Terminate button in operation panel for running ops (TerminateOperation).
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: running sync shows progress and can be terminated; conditions panel shows warnings.

### API-101 UI Settings — Repositories
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L171](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L171), [ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L312](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L312), [ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L320](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/repos-list/repos-list.tsx#L320), [ui/src/app/shared/services/repo-service.ts#L90](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/repo-service.ts#L90), [ui/src/app/shared/services/repocreds-service.ts#L60](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/repocreds-service.ts#L60)
- **Maturity**: GA (write credentials Beta)
- **Semantics**: Lists repositories with connection status (filters: type git/helm/all, status, project; Refresh list -> `forceRefresh=true`) and credential templates. `Connect Repo` panel with methods via SSH, via HTTP/HTTPS, via GitHub App, via Google Cloud, via Azure Service Principal; fields: type (git/helm/oci), name (mandatory for Helm), project, URL, credentials, TLS client cert/key, skip server verification, insecure HTTP only (OCI), enable LFS, enable OCI, proxy/no-proxy, depth, force HTTP basic auth, bearer token, Azure workload identity, "Save as write credential". Buttons `Connect` (create repository / write repository) and `Save as credentials template` (create repocreds / write repocreds). Row actions: Create application (prefills wizard), Disconnect/Remove.
- **Edge cases**: write variants shown only when hydrator enabled (server returns Unimplemented otherwise); errors per method shown as notifications.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: connect via HTTPS with creds; save credential template; disconnect; refresh connection status.

- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L38](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L38), [ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L85](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L85), [ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L99](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/certs-list/certs-list.tsx#L99)

- **Configuration surface**: none.

### API-104 UI Settings — Clusters (list and details)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/settings/components/clusters-list/clusters-list.tsx#L97](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/clusters-list/clusters-list.tsx#L97), [ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L30](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L30), [ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L40](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L40), [ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L66](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/cluster-details/cluster-details.tsx#L66)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: List (name, URL, version, connection status) with `Delete` (confirmation). Details page `/settings/clusters/:server` polls `ClusterService.Get` every second; editable NAME, NAMESPACES, LABELS, ANNOTATIONS (saved with `updatedFields`); read-only SERVER, CREDENTIALS TYPE, APPLICATIONS count, cache info; `Invalidate Cache` action.
- **Edge cases**: URL-encoded server in route (API-009).
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: rename cluster; invalidate cache; delete.

### API-105 UI Settings — Projects (list, details, roles, sync windows, events)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/settings/components/projects-list/projects-list.tsx#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/projects-list/projects-list.tsx#L24), [ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L903](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L903), [ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L169](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L169), [ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L360](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L360), [ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L871](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/settings/components/project-details/project-details.tsx#L871)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: List with `New Project` (name, description). Details (`GetDetailedProject`) tabs: Summary (editable general info, labels, source repositories, source namespaces, destinations, destination service accounts, cluster/namespace resource allow/deny lists, orphaned resources monitoring + warn + ignore list, signature keys; inherited global project values shown; LINKS from `project.links`), Roles (add/edit role: description, policies, groups, JWT tokens create/delete), Sync Windows (add/edit/delete windows incl. kind, schedule, duration, apps/namespaces/clusters, manual sync, time zone, AND operator, sync overrun), Events. Delete project action.
- **Edge cases**: update failures (e.g. apps would become invalid) shown as `Unable to update project`.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: create project; add destination; create role with token; add sync window; delete project.
