### API-108 UI User Info page (password change)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/user-info/components/user-info-overview/user-info-overview.tsx#L43](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/user-info/components/user-info-overview/user-info-overview.tsx#L43), [ui/src/app/user-info/components/user-info-overview/user-info-overview.tsx#L93](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/user-info/components/user-info-overview/user-info-overview.tsx#L93)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Shows Username, Issuer, Groups from userinfo. `Update Password` is offered only to logged-in users whose issuer is `argocd` (local); form Current / New / Confirm New Password (also openable via a URL query flag); calls `PUT /api/v1/account/password`; success notification `Your password has been successfully updated.`, failure `Unable to update your password.`
- **Edge cases**: mismatch confirmation -> client-side validation error; SSO users see no password action.
- **Configuration surface**: `passwordPattern`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: change password success; wrong current password error.

### API-109 UI Help page and CLI download links (`help.download.*`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L17](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L17), [ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L52](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L52), [ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L56](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/help/components/help.tsx#L56), [util/settings/settings.go#L1618](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1618), [docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L26](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/ui-customization.md#L26)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Help page with docs link (argo-cd.readthedocs.io), CLI downloads (always `download/argocd-linux` labeled with server arch from version Platform; plus one button per configured `help.download.<os>-<arch>` key among `darwin-amd64, darwin-arm64, windows-amd64, linux-amd64, linux-arm64, linux-ppc64le, linux-s390x`), and API docs link (`swagger-ui`).
- **Edge cases**: unknown os-arch keys ignored server-side.
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `help.download.<os>-<arch>`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: configured keys render buttons with correct icons/links; unknown key not rendered.

### API-110 UI instance banner (`ui.bannercontent`, `ui.bannerurl`, `ui.bannerpermanent`, `ui.bannerposition`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L49](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L49), [ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L117](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L117), [util/settings/settings.go#L1640](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1640), [util/settings/settings.go#L1648](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1648), [docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L315](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L315)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: When `uiBannerContent` is non-empty (only returned to logged-in users), a banner is shown on every layout page. `ui.bannerurl` makes the text a link (target `_blank`). Non-permanent banner offers "Dismiss for now" (session) and "Don't show again" (stores `hideBannerContent=<content>` preference; a changed content re-shows; empty content clears the preference). `ui.bannerpermanent: "true"` hides dismiss buttons and uses single-line height. `ui.bannerposition`: `top` (default when unset and content present), `bottom`, `both`.
- **Edge cases**: invalid banner URL logs a warning server-side but is still returned.
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `ui.bannercontent`, `ui.bannerurl`, `ui.bannerpermanent`, `ui.bannerposition`.
- **Exposure**: UI (+SettingsService).
- **Tests required**: banner positions; dismiss/don't-show-again behavior; content change re-shows; anonymous (not logged in) sees no banner.

### API-111 Help chat button (`help.chatUrl`, `help.chatText`)
- **Upstream**: [util/settings/settings.go#L1334](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1334), [ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L109](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/ui-banner/ui-banner.tsx#L109), [docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L40](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L40)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: If `help.chatUrl` is set, a fixed bottom-right button labeled `help.chatText` (default `Chat now!`) links to the URL (supports `title|url` form); invalid URL renders a disabled button with tooltip `Invalid URL provided`; position shifts above bottom banners.
- **Edge cases**: when `help.chatUrl` key is absent the server returns empty chat text.
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `help.chatUrl`, `help.chatText`.
- **Exposure**: UI (+SettingsService).
- **Tests required**: button shown with default text; invalid URL disabled.

### API-112 Custom styles (`ui.cssurl`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L201](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L201), [util/settings/settings.go#L1639](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1639), [docs/operator-manual/custom-styles.md#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/custom-styles.md#L24), [docs/operator-manual/custom-styles.md#L44](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/custom-styles.md#L44)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: If settings `uiCssURL` is non-empty the UI appends `<link rel="stylesheet" href="<uiCssURL>">` to `<head>` at bootstrap. Value may be an absolute URL or a path relative to the static assets dir (`/shared/app`, served via API-013 overlay), e.g. `./custom/my-styles.css`.
- **Edge cases**: CSS loaded for unauthenticated pages too (settings Get is auth-exempt).
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `ui.cssurl`; `--staticassets`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: stylesheet link injected; relative file served from overlay.

### API-113 Google Analytics integration (`ga.trackingid`, `ga.anonymizeusers`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L186](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L186), [util/settings/settings.go#L1328](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/util/settings/settings.go#L1328), [docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L35](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/argocd-cm.yaml#L35)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: If `ga.trackingid` set, UI initializes react-ga and records page views on every navigation; logged-in username is sent as userId hashed unless `ga.anonymizeusers: "false"` (default anonymize = true).
- **Edge cases**: no tracking when key empty.
- **Configuration surface**: argocd-cm `ga.trackingid`, `ga.anonymizeusers`.
- **Exposure**: UI (+SettingsService).
- **Tests required**: tracking initialized only when configured; hashed vs raw user id.

### API-114 UI status badge panel
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L9](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L9), [ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L22](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L22), [ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L47](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/components/badge-panel/badge-panel.tsx#L47), [docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/status-badge.md#L13)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Shown in app summary (and project details) only when `statusBadgeEnabled`. Badge URL root = `statusbadge.url` or current origin + base href; app badge `api/badge?name=<app>&revision=true&showAppName=true[&namespace=<ns> when apps-in-any-namespace]`, project badge `api/badge?project=<p>&revision=true`; snippet formats URL, Markdown, Textile, Rdoc, AsciiDoc with link to `applications/<app>` or `projects/<p>`.
- **Edge cases**: hidden when badge disabled.
- **Configuration surface**: `statusbadge.enabled`, `statusbadge.url`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: snippet text per format exactly as upstream.

### API-115 Managed-by URL annotation (`argocd.argoproj.io/managed-by-url`)
- **Upstream**: [server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L65](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go#L65), [server/application/application.go#L2349](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/application/application.go#L2349), [ui/src/app/shared/utils.ts#L44](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/utils.ts#L44), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-table-row.tsx#L32](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-table-row.tsx#L32), [docs/operator-manual/managed-by-url.md#L5](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/managed-by-url.md#L5)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: An Application annotated with a valid http(s) `argocd.argoproj.io/managed-by-url` is linked in the UI (e.g. app-of-apps child nodes, app links) to that Argo CD instance instead of the current one; the same value is exposed as `managedByURL` in application deep-link templates (defaulting to the instance `url` setting when absent/invalid).
- **Edge cases**: invalid URLs (non-http(s)) are ignored for deep links; in the UI the link is disabled with text `managed-by-url: invalid url provided` and a tooltip explaining it must be a valid http(s) URL.
- **Configuration surface**: annotation; argocd-cm `url`.
- **Exposure**: CRD annotation; UI; API (links).
- **Tests required**: child app link opens external instance URL; invalid value falls back.

### API-116 External URL links (`link.argocd.argoproj.io/*`, `argocd.argoproj.io/ignore-default-links`)
- **Upstream**: [common/common.go#L241](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L241), [common/common.go#L245](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/common/common.go#L245), [controller/cache/info.go#L45](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/controller/cache/info.go#L45), [pkg/apis/application/v1alpha1/types.go#L2162](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/pkg/apis/application/v1alpha1/types.go#L2162), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-urls.tsx#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-urls.tsx#L13), [docs/user-guide/external-url.md#L19](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/external-url.md#L19)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Any annotation key with prefix `link.argocd.argoproj.io/` on a managed resource adds its value (URL or `Title|URL`; the URL part must have scheme http or https per `ValidateExternalURL`, otherwise skipped) to the node's `networkingInfo.externalURLs`; Ingress (and similar) hosts are auto-added unless `argocd.argoproj.io/ignore-default-links: "true"`. Application-level annotations with the same prefix are added to `status.summary.externalURLs`. UI shows external-link icons on tree nodes and in the app list/tile; links with titles are listed first, then alphabetically.
- **Edge cases**: `javascript:`/`data:`/`vbscript:` URLs rejected on resources (XSS guard); application-level annotation values are added without that server-side check (UI still validates before rendering); `ignore-default-links` does not remove annotation links.
- **Configuration surface**: resource/application annotations.
- **Exposure**: UI (+API via status/tree).
- **Tests required**: annotated Deployment shows link; ignore-default-links hides ingress host; titled link label.

### API-117 Application notice annotations (`notice.argocd.argoproj.io/*`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L3](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L3), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L119](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L119), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L140](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-notice/notice.ts#L140), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1005](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L1005), [docs/user-guide/application-notice.md#L22](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/application-notice.md#L22)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Parsed client-side from Application annotations: `content` (required, trimmed non-empty; bidi override chars stripped; truncated to 500 chars with `…`), `severity` (`info` default | `warning` | `critical`; unknown -> info), `url` (only http/https; canonicalized), `permanent` (`"true"` disables dismiss), `icon` (allow-list `fa-info-circle, fa-exclamation-triangle, fa-exclamation-circle, fa-bell, fa-wrench, fa-clock, fa-bullhorn, fa-life-ring, fa-shield-alt`; else severity default), `scope` (`banner|icon|both`, default both). Banner on details page between status panel and tree; icon with tooltip (200-char preview) next to app name in tiles and list. Dismissals stored per user as `<ns>/<name>:<hash(content)>` (max 200 kept, oldest dropped).
- **Edge cases**: content change re-shows dismissed banner; `scope=icon` hides banner.
- **Configuration surface**: annotations `notice.argocd.argoproj.io/{content,severity,url,permanent,icon,scope}`.
- **Exposure**: CRD annotation; UI.
- **Tests required**: each annotation parsing rule (unit) and rendering; javascript: URL rejected; dismissal persistence.

### API-118 ApplicationSet web UI (list, details tree, preview)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L43](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L43), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L499](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L499), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L563](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-filter.tsx#L563), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/appset-preview-tab.tsx#L14](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/appset-preview-tab.tsx#L14), [ui/src/app/shared/services/applications-service.ts#L659](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/applications-service.ts#L659), [docs/operator-manual/feature-maturity.md#L28](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/feature-maturity.md#L28), [docs/user-guide/application-set-ui.md#L34](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/user-guide/application-set-ui.md#L34)
- **Maturity**: Alpha
- **Semantics**: `/applicationsets` list (tiles/table, search, health summary, favorites, filters: Favorites, HEALTH STATUS (Healthy/Progressing/Degraded/Unknown derived from appset status), LABELS), details page with status bar (health, conditions, last updated), resource tree (AppSet root + generated Applications from `ResourceTree`), slide-out SUMMARY / MANIFEST (read-only spec) / EVENTS / PREVIEW (sandbox edit of spec, `POST /api/v1/applicationsets/generate` and diff of DESIRED vs LIVE apps with DIFF/LIVE APPS/DESIRED APPS tabs; never persisted). Applications owned by an ApplicationSet show an owner badge linking to the AppSet and optional "Show parent ApplicationSet" tree toggle; AppSet nodes inside a parent app have a PREVIEW tab.
- **Edge cases**: preview requires `applicationsets, create` on the target project (server returns permission denied, shown as message).
- **Configuration surface**: none (feature always on in UI).
- **Exposure**: UI (+API-028).
- **Tests required**: list/filter/search; tree navigation to child app; preview diff after editing generator; permission-denied preview message.

### API-119 UI view preferences persistence
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L134](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L134), [ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L138](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L138), [ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L213](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/view-preferences-service.ts#L213)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: All UI preferences are stored in browser `localStorage` key `view_preferences` (JSON): app list (view default `tiles`, filters, favorites, page sizes, sort options, status bar), app details (view default `tree`, resource view `manifest`, pod sort `node`, group nodes, zoom, orphaned, hide managed fields, word wrap, dark mode, follow), appset list prefs, hidden banner content, dismissed notices, sidebar collapsed, theme (`auto`). Missing keys are merged with defaults.
- **Edge cases**: corrupted JSON falls back to defaults; preferences are per-browser, not per-user server-side.
- **Configuration surface**: browser storage.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: preference survives reload; clearing storage restores defaults.

### API-120 UI extensions — loading model and `window.extensionsAPI`
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L208](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L208), [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L9](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L9), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L4](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L4), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L14](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L14)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: The UI exposes `window.extensionsAPI = {registerResourceExtension, registerSystemLevelExtension, registerAppViewExtension, registerStatusPanelExtension, registerTopBarActionMenuExt}` and loads `/extensions.js` (API-050). Extensions must use the global `React` (not bundle their own). Registrations emit events so components rendered before registration update (event types `resource`, `systemLevel`, `appView`, `statusPanel`, `topBar`). Each extension component is rendered inside an error boundary where applicable.
- **Edge cases**: registration after initial render is supported via listeners.
- **Configuration surface**: files in `/tmp/extensions`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: extension script registering each type renders in the expected place.

### API-121 UI extension — resource tab (`registerResourceExtension`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L41](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L41), [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L186](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L186), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L163](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L163), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L33](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L33)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `registerResourceExtension(component, group, kind, tabTitle, opts?: {icon})`: adds a tab to the resource details panel for resources whose group and kind match the minimatch globs (`**` matches empty group); component props `{application, resource, tree}`; tabs sorted by title; group/kind `argoproj.io`/`Application` adds an app-level tab.
- **Edge cases**: extension errors are caught by an ErrorBoundary showing `Something went wrong with Extension for <kind>`.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: glob matching incl. `**` for core group; Application-level tab.

### API-122 UI extension — system level page (`registerSystemLevelExtension`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L47](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L47), [ui/src/app/app.tsx#L375](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/app.tsx#L375), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L71](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L71)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `registerSystemLevelExtension(component, title, path, icon)` adds a sidebar nav item (`fa <icon>`) and a route at `path` rendering the component inside a page titled `<title>` (document title `<title> - Argo CD`).
- **Edge cases**: path collisions with built-in routes are not prevented.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: nav item appears and route renders.

### API-123 UI extension — application view (`registerAppViewExtension`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L53](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L53), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L90](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L90), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L231](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L231)

- **Edge cases**: view preference stores the extension title.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.

### API-124 UI extension — status panel item (`registerStatusPanelExtension`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L59](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L59), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L95](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L95), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L104](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L104)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: `registerStatusPanelExtension(component, title, id, flyout?)` renders the component in the app status panel with props `{application, openFlyout}`; `openFlyout()` opens a sliding panel rendering `flyout` with `{application, tree}` (URL `extension=<id>`).
- **Edge cases**: without flyout, openFlyout is a no-op panel.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: flyout opens and closes.

### API-125 UI extension — top bar action menu (`registerTopBarActionMenuExt`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L65](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L65), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L100](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-details.tsx#L100), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L168](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L168)

- **Edge cases**: narrow screens collapse labels.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.

### API-127 UI resource icon resolution order (`ResourceIcon`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L24), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L25](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L25), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L28](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L28), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L35](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L35), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L42), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L46](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L46), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L79](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L79), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L54](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L54), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L105](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L105), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L124](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L124), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L146](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L146), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L496](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L496), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-pod-view/pod-view.tsx#L129](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-pod-view/pod-view.tsx#L129), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L310](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L310), [docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L1)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Every place the UI draws a Kubernetes resource renders `ResourceIcon({group, kind, customStyle?})` in a 40px x 32px box: resource tree nodes (regular, grouped-node and pod-group nodes, network view), the List view kind column, the Pods view group headers, the resource details panel header and the ApplicationSet details header. Resolution is first-match, in this order:
  1. `kind === 'node'` (lowercase; the synthetic node group in the Pods view) -> `<img src="assets/images/infrastructure_components/node.svg">`.
  2. `kind === 'Application'` -> font icon `<i class="icon argo-icon-application resource-icon__font-icon">` (font-size 32px), whatever the group.
  3. `kind === 'ApplicationSet'` -> font icon `argo-icon-applicationset`, whatever the group.
  4. Non-empty `group`: exact key lookup in the API-group registry (API-128); on a miss, iterate registry keys in registry order and take the first key that contains `*` and satisfies `minimatch(group, key)`. The matched key becomes a path segment by replacing every `*` with `_`, and the icon is `assets/images/resources/<segment>/icon.svg`.
  5. Kind-only lookup in the built-in kind map (API-129) -> `assets/images/resources/<short>.svg`. This applies to the core group and to any group with no custom icon (e.g. `networking.k8s.io`/`Ingress` -> `ing.svg`).
  6. Fallback: a grey (`#8FA4B1`) 32px circle showing `kind` with all ASCII lowercase letters removed (`UnknownResource` -> `UR`, `MyCustomKind` -> `MCK`) in white; font 1em with `2px 4px` padding when the result has 2 characters or fewer, otherwise 0.6em with `0px 4px`.
  `alt`/`title` is the kind. Asset URLs are relative and resolve against the page `<base href>`, so they follow `--basehref`/`--rootpath`.
- **Edge cases**: a group icon wins over a kind icon (a kind named `Deployment` in `cert-manager.io` gets the cert-manager icon). Any resource whose kind is `Application`/`ApplicationSet` gets the Argo icon even when it is not `argoproj.io`. Capitalized `Node` is neither `node` nor in the kind map, so it shows initials `N`. A kind with no uppercase letters gives empty initials, an empty grey circle. Digits and uppercase/non-ASCII characters are kept in the initials (`V1Beta` -> `V1B`). Tree pod-group nodes and Pods-view groups pass `'Unknown'` when the kind is empty (initials `U`). A wildcard key `*.example.com` does not match the bare group `example.com`, because minimatch needs the literal `.example.com` suffix (see API-128).
- **Configuration surface**: none at runtime. Icons are compiled into the UI bundle; no ConfigMap key, annotation or env var changes them.
- **Exposure**: UI (resource tree, network view, List view, Pods view, resource details panel, ApplicationSet details).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `('', 'ConfigMap')` -> `assets/images/resources/cm.svg`; `('networking.k8s.io', 'Ingress')` -> `ing.svg`; `('cert-manager.io', 'Certificate')` -> `cert-manager.io/icon.svg`; `('pkg.crossplane.io', 'Provider')` and `('identify.provider.crossplane.io', 'Provider')` -> `_.crossplane.io/icon.svg`; `('promoter.argoproj.io', 'PromotionStrategy')` -> `promoter.argoproj.io/icon.svg`; `('view.promoter.argoproj.io', ...)` -> `_.promoter.argoproj.io/icon.svg`.
  - Special cases: `node`, `Application` (empty group) and `ApplicationSet` (`argoproj.io`) render the special icon in a 40x32 box, with 32px font-size for the font icons.
  - Fallback: `('unknown.example.io', 'UnknownResource')` renders no `<img>` and the text `UR` in a 40x32 outer box; `('', 'MyCustomKind')` -> `MCK`; an all-lowercase kind renders without error.
  - Precedence: when both a group icon and a kind icon exist, the group icon is used.
  - Recovery: with the UI served under a non-root base href, icon assets still load (no 404).

### API-128 UI bundled API-group icon registry (`resourceIconGroups`, `_.` wildcard directories, `make resourceiconsgen`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-customizations.ts#L6](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-customizations.ts#L6), [hack/generate-icons-typescript.sh#L13](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/hack/generate-icons-typescript.sh#L13), [Makefile#L284](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/Makefile#L284), [Makefile#L289](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/Makefile#L289), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L79](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L79), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L87](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L87), [ui/src/assets/images/resources/_.crossplane.io/icon.svg#L1](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/assets/images/resources/_.crossplane.io/icon.svg#L1), [docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L7](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L7), [docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L9](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L9), [docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L15](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md#L15)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: The registry is a generated TypeScript object `resourceIconGroups: {[groupGlob]: true}` with one entry per `ui/src/assets/images/resources/**/icon.svg`, sorted by path, in which the first `_` of each directory name becomes `*`. At v3.5.5 it has exactly 22 entries. Wildcards, tried in this order after an exact miss: `*.crossplane.io`, `*.fluxcd.io`, `*.knative.dev`, `*.promoter.argoproj.io`. Exact groups: `cassandra.rook.io`, `cert-manager.io`, `core.spinkube.dev`, `external-secrets.io`, `flagger.app`, `install.istio.io`, `jaegertracing.io`, `k8s.keycloak.org`, `kafka.strimzi.io`, `keda.sh`, `kubevirt.io`, `kyverno.io`, `nauth.io`, `opentelemetry.io`, `projectcontour.io`, `promoter.argoproj.io`, `work.karmada.io`, `zookeeper.pravega.io`. In the wildcards, `*` matches any run of characters without `/`, dots included (`identify.provider.crossplane.io` matches `*.crossplane.io`). The contribution guide requires an Apache-2.0-compatible license and the monochrome colour `#8fa4b1`. `make resourceiconsgen` (also run by `make codegen-local`) regenerates the file. The `ui/src/assets/images/resources/` directory holds 52 entries: the 22 group directories plus the 30 built-in kind SVGs (API-129).
- **Edge cases**: `*.<suffix>` does not match the bare `<suffix>` group, so `crossplane.io`, `fluxcd.io` and `knative.dev` fall through to kind or initials. `promoter.argoproj.io` is covered only because it also has an exact directory. The generator replaces only the first `_` of a directory name, while the runtime turns every `*` back into `_` (global replace), so the asset path stays consistent. A directory with an underscore in its real group name (none exist) would wrongly become a glob. Directories without `icon.svg` are ignored. Exact matches win over wildcards. When several wildcards match, the first in registry (sorted) order wins. The set is fixed at UI build time; an operator cannot add icons without rebuilding the UI.
- **Configuration surface**: build-time only: `ui/src/assets/images/resources/<group>/icon.svg` and `ui/src/assets/images/resources/_.<suffix>/icon.svg`, plus `make resourceiconsgen`.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: each of the 22 groups renders its own icon. A sub-group per wildcard matches (e.g. `apiextensions.crossplane.io`, `source.toolkit.fluxcd.io`, `serving.knative.dev`, `view.promoter.argoproj.io`).
  - Negative: the bare suffix groups `crossplane.io`, `fluxcd.io`, `knative.dev` do not get the wildcard icon. A group from outside the registry (e.g. `apps`) falls through to the kind map.
  - Consistency: every registry key, converted (`*` -> `_`), resolves to an existing `icon.svg` asset that is served with HTTP 200.

### API-129 UI built-in kind icon map (`resourceIcons`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L4](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L4), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L26](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L26), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.tsx#L42), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L36](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-icon.test.tsx#L36)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Used when no special kind and no group icon matched. Lookup is by `kind` only, case-sensitive and group-agnostic, and yields `assets/images/resources/<short>.svg` with 2px padding. There are 32 entries: ClusterRole `c-role`, ConfigMap `cm`, ClusterRoleBinding `crb`, CustomResourceDefinition `crd`, CronJob `cronjob`, Deployment `deploy`, DaemonSet `ds`, Endpoint/Endpoints/EndpointSlice `ep`, Group `group`, HorizontalPodAutoscaler `hpa`, Ingress `ing`, Job `job`, LimitRange `limits`, NetworkPolicy `netpol`, Namespace `ns`, Pod `pod`, PodSecurityPolicy `psp`, PersistentVolume `pv`, PersistentVolumeClaim `pvc`, `Quote` `quota`, RoleBinding `rb`, Role `role`, ReplicaSet `rs`, ServiceAccount `sa`, StorageClass `sc`, Secret `secret`, StatefulSet `sts`, Service `svc`, User `user`, Volume `vol`.
- **Edge cases**: the quota icon is keyed `Quote`, not `ResourceQuota`, so a `ResourceQuota` shows initials `RQ` while its text label is `quota` (API-130). The baseline renders it this way. Kinds that have a label but no icon render initials: ComponentStatus, CertificateSigningRequest, Event, Node, PodDisruptionBudget, PriorityClass, ReplicationController, ResourceQuota. A CRD kind that collides with a built-in name (e.g. `Group`, `User`, `Volume`) in a group with no custom icon gets the built-in icon. Lowercase kind strings (e.g. `deployment`) do not match.
- **Configuration surface**: none (compiled in).
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: each of the 32 kinds, with an empty group and with a non-registry group (e.g. `apps/Deployment`, `batch/CronJob`), resolves to its SVG.
  - Negative: `ResourceQuota` renders initials `RQ`; `PodDisruptionBudget` renders `PDB`; a lowercase `deployment` renders no `<img>`.

### API-130 UI resource kind abbreviation labels (`ResourceLabel`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-label.ts#L3](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-label.ts#L3), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L39](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts#L39), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L301](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L301), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L831](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-resource-tree/application-resource-tree.tsx#L831), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-resource-list.tsx#L191](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-resource-list.tsx#L191), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-pod-view/pod-view.tsx#L131](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-pod-view/pod-view.tsx#L131), [ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L311](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/resource-details/resource-details.tsx#L311), [ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-resource-filter.tsx#L148](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/application-details/application-resource-filter.tsx#L148)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Under each resource icon the UI prints a short kind caption: `resources.get(kind) || kind.toLowerCase()`. The `resources` map has 38 entries. It holds the icon-map kinds except `Quote` and `EndpointSlice`, plus ComponentStatus `cs`, CertificateSigningRequest `csr`, Event `ev`, Node `no`, PodDisruptionBudget `pdb`, PriorityClass `pc`, ReplicationController `rc` and ResourceQuota `quota`. The caption appears on tree nodes (not on the root node, `!rootNode`), grouped-node tree entries, the List view kind column, Pods view group headers, the resource details panel header and the ApplicationSet details header. The same map is passed as `abbreviations` to the KINDS autocomplete in the application-details Resource filters.
- **Edge cases**: an unknown kind is lowercased (`Certificate` -> `certificate`). `EndpointSlice` has an icon (`ep.svg`) but no abbreviation, so its caption is `endpointslice`. An empty kind yields an empty caption. An `undefined` kind is not guarded: `kind.toLowerCase()` would throw, and the callers pass the raw kind. The root node of the tree shows no caption.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**: `Deployment` -> `deploy`, `ResourceQuota` -> `quota`, `PodDisruptionBudget` -> `pdb`, `Certificate` -> `certificate`; the root node has no caption; typing an abbreviation in the KINDS filter offers the full kind (see Unconfirmed).

### API-131 UI extension host globals and script load order (`window.React`, `window.ReactDOM`, `window.ReactJSXRuntime`, `window.Moment`)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/index.tsx#L19](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/index.tsx#L19), [ui/src/app/index.tsx#L22](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/index.tsx#L22), [ui/src/app/index.html#L24](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/index.html#L24), [ui/src/app/webpack.config.js#L104](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/webpack.config.js#L104), [ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L208](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/shared/services/extensions-service.ts#L208), [server/server.go#L1331](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1331), [server/server.go#L1345](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/server/server.go#L1345), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L67](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L67), [docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L17](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md#L17)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Besides `window.extensionsAPI` and the legacy `window.extensions` (API-120, API-126), the host UI defines four module globals for extensions to use as bundler `externals`:

  | Module | Global | Host version (v3.5.5 lockfile) |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | `react` | `window.React` | 19.2.6 |
  | `react-dom` (main entry, not `react-dom/client`) | `window.ReactDOM` | 19.2.6 |
  | `react/jsx-runtime` | `window.ReactJSXRuntime` | 19.2.6 |
  | `moment` | `window.Moment` | 2.30.1 |

  They are assigned in the main bundle entry (`index.tsx`) synchronously, right after `createRoot(...).render(<App/>)` is scheduled. `/extensions.js` (API-050) is loaded by `<script defer src="extensions.js">` placed after `</body>`, so it runs after the main bundle, and the globals and `extensionsAPI` already exist when extension code evaluates. Extensions are expected not to bundle their own React, ReactDOM, JSX runtime or moment, so their components share the host React instance and its hooks and context.
- **Edge cases**: the server wraps each file in its own `try { ... } catch(e) { console.error('Extension <file>.js failed to load:', e); }` (`<file>` is the base file name), so a synchronous runtime exception during one file's evaluation is logged and later files still run. All files are concatenated into one script, though, so a syntax error in any file is a parse error for the whole `/extensions.js`, and then no extension loads. Exceptions thrown later (render, event handlers, promises) are not caught by this wrapper; render errors in resource tabs hit the ErrorBoundary (API-121). `react-dom/client` (`createRoot`) is not exposed as a global. The host itself compiles JSX in classic mode (`"jsx": "react"`), but still exposes the automatic runtime for extensions. The `// source:` comment emitted per file repeats the base name after the full path (`<path>/<name>`), which is cosmetic.
- **Configuration surface**: none (globals are hard-coded); extension files in `/tmp/extensions` (API-050).
- **Exposure**: UI (+ HTTP `/extensions.js`).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: after page load, `window.React.createElement`, `window.ReactJSXRuntime.jsx`/`jsxs`/`Fragment`, `window.ReactDOM.createPortal` and `window.Moment` are defined, and `window.React.version` starts with `19.`. An extension built with externals `{react: 'React', 'react-dom': 'ReactDOM', 'react/jsx-runtime': 'ReactJSXRuntime', moment: 'Moment'}` that uses hooks registers a resource tab that renders and updates.
  - Ordering: an extension that reads `window.React` and calls `window.extensionsAPI.register*` at top level runs without `undefined` errors.
  - Negative/isolation: `extension-a.js` throwing at top level logs `Extension extension-a.js failed to load:` and `extension-b.js` still registers. A file with a syntax error stops all extensions from loading (baseline parity), and the core UI keeps working.

### API-132 UI React 19 extension compatibility contract (`react/jsx-runtime` externalization, 3.5 breaking change)
- **Upstream**: [ui/package.json#L37](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/package.json#L37), [ui/package.json#L40](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/package.json#L40), [ui/pnpm-lock.yaml#L85](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/pnpm-lock.yaml#L85), [ui/src/app/index.tsx#L22](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/index.tsx#L22), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/3.4-3.5.md#L46](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/3.4-3.5.md#L46), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L3](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L3), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L10](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L10), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L38](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L38), [docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L51](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md#L51)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: Since 3.5 the host UI runs React 19 (`react`/`react-dom` `^19.0.0`, resolved to 19.2.6); 3.4 ran React 16. The extension ABI is therefore "React 19 host instance". Extensions must externalize `react`, `react-dom`, `react/jsx-runtime` and `moment` to the globals in API-131. An extension (or a dependency such as `antd`) that bundles an older `react/jsx-runtime` reaches into a React internals object that React 19 removed. It throws a `TypeError` at script evaluation, before rendering, surfaced as `Extension <name>.js failed to load: TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading '<prop>')`. Rebuilding with `'react/jsx-runtime': 'ReactJSXRuntime'` in `externals` fixes it. For Patrol, extensions built for Argo CD 3.5 must load unmodified: same React major, same global names, same failure isolation.
- **Edge cases**: the `<prop>` name and stack frames vary with minification, so detection relies on (a) a load-time `TypeError` and (b) a missing `react/jsx-runtime` external. Extensions still failing after externalizing likely depend on a library version that is incompatible with React 19 and must bump it. Other extensions and the core UI keep working when one extension fails (per-file try/catch). Extensions that only use `React.createElement` through the `React` global are not affected by the jsx-runtime issue. A host that downgraded or upgraded the React major would break the published extension ecosystem.
- **Configuration surface**: extension build `externals` (outside Argo CD); no server config.
- **Exposure**: UI.
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: a React 19 extension that uses the automatic JSX runtime, with `react/jsx-runtime` externalized, loads and renders (resource tab, status panel, top-bar and system-level types). A classic `React.createElement` extension loads.
  - Negative: an extension that bundles a pre-React-19 `react/jsx-runtime` fails at load with a `TypeError`, logged with the prefix `Extension <name>.js failed to load:`, and does not register anything.
  - Recovery: in the same `/tmp/extensions`, a compatible extension next to the failing one still registers and renders, and core pages keep working.

- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L146](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L146), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L158](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L158), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L411](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L411)
- **Maturity**: GA (ApplicationSets list page itself is Alpha, see API-118)
- **Semantics**: Both `/applications` and `/applicationsets` wrap the page in an argo-ui `KeybindingProvider`. The search box shows a `/` keyboard hint. Pressing `/` focuses the search input, and focusing selects any existing text. Pressing `Esc` blurs the input only if it was focused through the `/` binding (internal `isFocused` flag). On the Applications list both bindings are disabled while the New App panel is open (`?new=` query parameter present); the ApplicationSets list has no such guard. Search text is URL-synchronized (`search=`) as described in API-089. Choosing an autocomplete suggestion navigates to that app or appset.
- **Edge cases**: focusing the input by mouse does not set `isFocused`, so `Esc` does not blur it through this binding. After a `/` focus followed by a mouse blur, `isFocused` stays true, and the next `Esc` calls `blur()` on an unfocused input and reports the key as handled. The Summary view (Applications list) keeps the search bindings but has no item navigation.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI (Applications list, ApplicationSets list).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: on each list, `/` focuses the search input and selects its text; `Esc` afterwards blurs it.
  - Negative: with `?new=...` (New App panel open) on `/applications`, `/` does not move focus to the list search.
  - Typing `/` inside a different focused text field: behavior follows the argo-ui provider (see Unconfirmed). Parity test records the baseline.

### API-134 UI tiles view keyboard navigation (arrows, Enter, Esc, digit and numpad keys)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L21](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L21), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L49](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L49), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L58](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L58), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L63](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L63), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L74](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L74), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L85](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L85), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L92](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx#L92), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L268](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L268), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L300](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L300), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-tile.tsx#L72](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-tile.tsx#L72), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.scss#L42](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.scss#L42), [ui/src/app/applications/components/utils.tsx#L1939](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/utils.tsx#L1939)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: In the tiles view of the Applications list and the ApplicationSets list, a selection cursor (argo-ui `useNav(items.length)`, initially -1 = none) covers the items of the current page only. The selected tile gets class `applications-tiles__selected` (1px teal outline).
  - `Right`/`Left` move the cursor by +1/-1.
  - `Down`/`Up` move it by +/- tiles-per-row. Tiles-per-row is `floor(containerWidth / firstTileWidth)` (minimum 1), recomputed on window resize after a 1s debounce.
  - `Enter`, with an item selected, navigates to `/applications/[<ns>/]<name>` or `/applicationsets/[<ns>/]<name>` (`getAppUrl`).
  - `Esc`, with an item selected, clears the selection.
  - Digit keys (`NumKey`) and numpad digit keys (`NumPadKey`) call `reset()` and then `nav(NumKeyToNumber(key))`, which jumps to the Nth item (1-based from no selection).
  Each binding reports whether it handled the key (`Enter`/`Esc` return false when nothing is selected).
- **Edge cases**: moves that would leave `[0, length)` are rejected by `useNav`, so the cursor stays put (no wrap). Selection is page-local, and changing page, filters or the live app stream changes `length` and can leave a stale index. Number jumps use the cursor value captured before `reset()` runs, so with an existing selection they may move relative to it instead of absolutely (argo-ui state semantics, see Unconfirmed). From an empty selection, a digit larger than the page size selects nothing, and so does `0` if `NumKeyToNumber` maps it to 0. Tiles-per-row starts at 1 until the first measurement completes (up to 1s), so the first `Down` may move by one tile.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI (Applications list tiles view, ApplicationSets list tiles view).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: with 3 tiles per row, `Right` x2 selects tile 3 and `Down` selects tile 6. `Enter` opens that app's details URL (namespaced path when the app has a namespace). `Esc` clears the highlight. Pressing `3` with nothing selected highlights the third tile; numpad `3` does the same.
  - Negative: `Left` on the first tile and `Down` on the last row keep the selection. `Enter` with nothing selected does nothing.
  - Same behavior on the ApplicationSets tiles view.

### API-135 UI table (list) view keyboard navigation (Up/Down, Enter, Esc)
- **Upstream**: [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L20](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L20), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L25](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L25), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L27](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L27), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L34](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-table.tsx#L34), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L339](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L339), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L344](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-sets-list.tsx#L344), [ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-table-row.tsx#L67](https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/blob/a5038b0e2bbac1715754380ae0084df8e3d5b48c/ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/application-table-row.tsx#L67)
- **Maturity**: GA
- **Semantics**: In the table (`list`) view of the Applications list and the ApplicationSets list, `Down`/`Up` move the row cursor by +1/-1 within the current page (initially no selection). The selected row gets the `applications-tiles__selected` class. `Enter` with a selection opens the item's details page (`getAppUrl`). `Esc` always resets the cursor and reports handled only if something was selected. Unlike tiles, the table view binds no `Left`/`Right` and no digit/numpad keys.
- **Edge cases**: no wrap at either end. Pressing `Down` from no selection selects row 1. The Applications Summary view has no navigation bindings.
- **Configuration surface**: none.
- **Exposure**: UI (Applications list table view, ApplicationSets list table view).
- **Tests required**:
  - Positive: `Down` x2 highlights row 2; `Enter` opens it; `Esc` clears the highlight.
  - Negative: digit keys and `Left`/`Right` do not change the selection in table view; `Up` from row 1 stays on row 1.

## Unconfirmed

- REST server-streaming wire format details (exact SSE framing, heartbeat behavior, `{"result": ...}` envelope, and how `fields=` projection is applied for `ProjectService.List`, `ApplicationService.ManagedResources` and stream forwarders) are implemented in the external module `github.com/argoproj/pkg/v2/grpc/http` (`StreamForwarder`, `NewStreamForwarder`, `UnaryForwarder`, `UnaryForwarderWithFieldProcessor`), which is not vendored in the checkout. Inferred from UI usage (`result.type`, `result.application.*` fields; `EventSource`). Paths checked: `pkg/apiclient/application/forwarder_overwrite.go`, `pkg/apiclient/applicationset/forwarder_overwrite.go`, `pkg/apiclient/project/forwarder_overwrite.go`, `ui/src/app/shared/services/requests.ts`, `ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-list.tsx`.
- `cluster.links` deep links (named in the task scope) do not exist at this commit: only `application.links`, `resource.links`, `project.links` are read (`util/settings/settings.go` L554-559); cluster data is only available as the `cluster` template variable. Paths checked: `util/settings/settings.go`, `server/deeplinks/deeplinks.go`, `docs/operator-manual/deep_links.md`, `ui/src/app` (no `cluster.links` reference).
- A top-level `argocd bcrypt` command does not exist; `bcrypt` is `argocd account bcrypt` (`cmd/argocd/commands/account.go` L66, `cmd/argocd/commands/root.go`).
- "Help chat" is only the `help.chatUrl` floating button (API-111); no other chat integration found in `ui/src/app`.
- `GetManifestsWithFiles` REST binding (`POST /api/v1/applications/manifestsWithFiles`) for a client-streaming RPC: behavior of grpc-gateway for client streams over REST was not exercised; CLI uses native gRPC. Path checked: `server/application/application.proto` L410.
- Exact behavior of `/auth/logout`, `/auth/login`, `/auth/callback`, `/api/dex`, `/api/webhook` is owned by the SSO/session and webhook areas; only their registration is inventoried here (`server/server.go` L1182, L1257, L1263, L1367).
- Server-side TLS certificate source (`argocd-server-tls` vs `argocd-secret`) not traced here; owned by the TLS/security area (`server/server.go` L649 uses `server.settings.Certificate`).
- UI details of notification subscriptions editor (annotation format `notifications.argoproj.io/subscribe.<trigger>.<service>`) inferred from component names; checked `ui/src/app/applications/components/application-summary/edit-notification-subscriptions.tsx` imports only.
- Keyboard plumbing for API-133/134/135 lives in the external `argo-ui/v2` package (`KeybindingProvider`, `KeybindingContext.useKeybinding`, `useNav`, `Key`, `NumKey`, `NumPadKey`, `NumKeyToNumber`, pinned by `ui/package.json` L19 to argo-ui commit `c089d1d2d84df87f3712ae661273a5ab0d1ef3b0`), which is not vendored (no `ui/node_modules`). Not confirmed: whether bindings are suppressed while an `<input>` has focus; whether a handler returning `true` stops other handlers bound to the same key (e.g. search-bar `Esc` vs tiles `Esc`); the exact bounds and clamping in `useNav`; the `NumKey` to number mapping (including `0`); and whether digit jumps are absolute or relative to an existing selection. The descriptions in API-134 are inferred from call sites (`reset(); return nav(NumKeyToNumber(n))`). Paths checked: `ui/src/app/applications/components/applications-list/applications-tiles.tsx`, `applications-table.tsx`, `application-sets-list.tsx`, `applications-list.tsx`, `ui/package.json`.
- How the KINDS resource-filter autocomplete uses `abbreviations` (API-130), e.g. whether typing `deploy` suggests `Deployment`, is implemented in the argo-ui v2 `Autocomplete` component, which is not vendored. Paths checked: `ui/src/app/applications/components/filter/filter.tsx` L178, `application-resource-filter.tsx` L148.
- Main-bundle-before-extension ordering (API-131) is inferred from `html-webpack-plugin` ^5.6.0 defaults (`scriptLoading: 'defer'`, injected in `<head>`), because `ui/src/app/webpack.config.js` L104 passes only `template`. No built `ui/dist/app/index.html` exists in the checkout to confirm the emitted script tags.
- Exact export surface of `window.ReactDOM` (`react-dom@19.2.6` main entry) and whether `window.Moment` (assigned from `import * as Moment from 'moment'` and compiled by esbuild-loader) is directly callable or needs `.default`: not verified without `node_modules`. Paths checked: `ui/src/app/index.tsx` L2-4 and L19-22, `ui/pnpm-lock.yaml` L73-96, `ui/src/app/tsconfig.json`.

## Doc/implementation differences

- Proxy extensions maturity: `docs/operator-manual/feature-maturity.md` L21 and `proxy-extensions.md` L3-7 say **Beta**, but `proxy-extensions.md` L21 still says "As proxy extension is in Alpha phase" and `server/server.go` L1229 comments "Proxy extension is currently an alpha feature". Baseline treats it as Beta (feature-maturity table).
- Proxy extension headers: `proxy-extensions.md` L165-166 states "Reserved header names will be ignored" for `extensions.backend.services.headers`; no such filtering exists in `server/extension/extension.go` — configured headers are set in the reverse-proxy Director after `prepareRequest`, so they can override `Argocd-*` headers (L537-543 vs L837-856).
- UI extensions: docs declare `registerSystemLevelExtension(component, title, options: {icon?})` (`ui-extensions.md` L71) but code signature is `(component, title, path, icon)` (`extensions-service.ts` L47); the doc example itself passes four positional args.
- Deep links: `deep_links.md` L48 says `application.links` can use `app/application` and `cluster`, but its own example (L63) uses `{{.project.metadata.labels.env}}` inside `application.links`; code passes no `project` object for application links (`server/application/application.go` L2349 `CreateDeepLinksObject(nil, obj, clstObj, nil)`), so that example renders `<no value>`/fails. Code additionally exposes an undocumented `managedByURL` key.
- Status badge: `status-badge.md` does not document the `project` (repeatable, aggregate) and `namespace` query parameters that `server/badge/badge.go` L110-170 supports; the doc says `revision` shows the revision "targeted by the application", while code shows the last **synced** revision from `status.operationState.syncResult` (L134-139).
- ApplicationSet UI: `application-set-ui.md` L34-36 lists filters by project, namespace, labels and health; code `AppSetsFilter` (`applications-filter.tsx` L563-571) provides Favorites, Health and Labels only.
- `argocd completion`: help text says supported shells are bash, zsh and fish (also in the invalid-shell error message), but `powershell` is implemented (`cmd/argocd/commands/completion.go` L245-249).
- gRPC message size: code comment says 100MB (`server/server.go` L925-927) while the effective default is 200MB (`pkg/apiclient/apiclient.go` L68).
- Sensitive-method log redaction lists `/gpgkey.GPGKeyService/CreateGnuPGPublicKey` (`server/server.go` L942), which is not an existing RPC (actual: `/gpgkey.GPGKeyService/Create`), so GPG create payloads are not redacted.
- `GnuPGPublicKeyCreateRequest.upsert` is documented in the proto (`server/gpgkey/gpgkey.proto`) but ignored by `server/gpgkey/gpgkey.go` L72-98.
- `NotificationService.ListTriggers` swallows non-NotFound config errors and dereferences a nil API (`server/notification/notification.go` L24-37), unlike `ListServices`/`ListTemplates` which return the error.
- `ListWriteRepositoryCredentials` enumerates read-credential URLs and filters write creds by non-empty password (`server/repocreds/repocreds.go` L60-81), so SSH/GitHub-App-only write credential templates are not listed.
- CLI generated docs show the `--config` default as `/home/user/.config/argocd/config` (`docs/user-guide/commands/argocd.md`), whereas the real default is resolved at runtime (`ARGOCD_CONFIG_DIR`, legacy `~/.argocd`, `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/argocd`, `~/.config/argocd`; `util/localconfig/localconfig.go` L270-297).
- Custom resource icons: `docs/developer-guide/custom-resource-icons.md` L15-18 says a `_.example.com` directory applies to both `example.com` and `another.example.com`. The code (`resource-icon.tsx` L86-88) uses `minimatch(group, '*.example.com')`, which does not match the bare `example.com`, which is why the bundle ships both `promoter.argoproj.io` and `_.promoter.argoproj.io`.
- `hack/generate-icons-typescript.sh` L3-4 says "Users may configure custom resource icons in resource_customizations". The script reads only `ui/src/assets/images/resources/**/icon.svg`, `resource_customizations/` contains no SVGs, and there is no runtime icon configuration: icons are build-time only.
- UI extensions externals: `docs/developer-guide/extensions/ui-extensions.md` L17-24 says only to avoid bundling React and to "use the `react` global variable" (the actual global is `React`), with the snippet `externals: { react: "React"; }`, which is not a valid object literal. It does not mention `react-dom`, `react/jsx-runtime` or `moment`. Since 3.5 the code exposes `ReactDOM`, `ReactJSXRuntime` and `Moment` (`ui/src/app/index.tsx` L19-22), and `docs/operator-manual/upgrading/ui-extensions-react-19-upgrading.md` requires `react/jsx-runtime` to be externalized or the extension fails to load.
- Built-in kind icon map keys the quota icon as `Quote` (`ui/src/app/applications/components/resources.ts` L26) instead of `ResourceQuota`. The label map uses `ResourceQuota` (L67), so ResourceQuota resources show initials `RQ` with caption `quota`. This is a code-internal inconsistency, not a doc conflict, recorded for parity.
